Chapter 2801: Human Nature Laboratory

“You seem to be ina lot of pain. Is it really okay?”

Li Yao probed carefully, “What did you mean when you mentioned ‘do you know that you are being controlled’ in the meeting just now?

“Also, when I was searching for the files of ‘Black Wind King Li Yao’ and the damage he caused on Black Castle, I found some unusual intelligence. Holy Light Academy, Chu Zhiling, Chu Zhiyun… They seem to be related to the major. Do you know anything about them?”

Chu Zhixiao was silent. He almost smoked a cigarette before he put it out.

She jumped off the window and kneeled in front of Li Yao, observing him carefully.

Li Yao suddenly became nervous, thinking that he had exposed something.

“The memory module is seriously damaged.”

Chu Zhixiao asked, “Have you forgotten everything that I explained to you?”

Li Yao was somewhat relieved. He said, “I don’t know what the major meant. But I do feel that everybody in the yaksha squad, including the major, is in great pain, as if somebody bullied you, and that somebody is not the puppet king.

“You must’ve stayed in the Holy Light Academy before, didn’t you, Major? It seems that the place is not as simple as raising ‘Purifiers’. Has anybody ever bullied you there? Hehe. I will protect you. You will not be bullied by bad guys!”

Chu Zhixiao was dazed for a long time, with a bitter smile on his face.

“Thank you, Red Pig.”

She stuck her forehead to the cold shell of the spider tank and said softly, “You are still the same as before. Every time your memory chip is seriously damaged in a fierce battle, you ask the same question, and you cannot wait to protect me.

“It’s true that a lot of things happened to me in the Holy Light Institute, and I may have experienced some pain, but… I’ve forgotten most of them. No bad guys bullied me on purpose.

“Tdon’t understand.”

Li Yao asked, “If no bad guys bullied you, why are you in so much pain?”

“There are some things that are hard to explain to you.

Looking at his twisted self reflected in the shell of the spider tank that was as smooth as a mirror, Chu Zhixiao said in a daze, “We live in a world of smoke and evilness. In this world, order is the most important thing, Only absolute order can allow everyone to live in peace and harmony.

“And in order to defend the precious peace and harmony, in order to prevent the sacred order from being destroyed by the evil doctrines, some people had to stand up, endure the greatest pain, pay the greatest sacrifices, receive the blessings of the gods, and become the first and last line of defense in the

darkness, fighting against the unimaginable devils. This is my duty and my destiny. Do you understand?”

“I don’t know.”

Li Yao asked, “But why does it have to be a major?”

“If it was not me, then who was it?

Chu Zhixiao smiled and said, “In fact, there’s no ‘should’ who it is. If I didn’t have to bear the pain, there would be a second person, maybe my brothers and sisters. If nobody could bear the pain and nobody was willing to fight for order, the sacred order would collapse. The orderly ‘soldiers’ and ‘workers’

would turn into ‘disillusioners’, ‘fanatics’, and ‘ghouls’. Such a world would be a hundred times worse than it is right now!

“Therefore, since it was my destiny, however painful and difficult it was, I could only grit my teeth and carry on. I could not shift the responsibility to the next ‘Slaughter Goddess’. That was all.

“AR”

Li Yao’s heart was beating fast, and he did not know what to say. “It turns out that the major knows everything about your past. I thought that you had forgotten a lot of things and even yourself!”

“No. I remember clearly that I went through a lot of ‘purification’ and erased a lot of my memories.

Chu Zhixiao pointed at his temples and said, “Did you know that the human brain has the function of erasing memories regularly? Most people will forget 99% of their life. It is a certain… self-protection mechanism of the soul. Nobody can move forward with every second of the past on their back. Those

who suffer from ‘hyperthymesia’ and remember everything clearly are living in excruciating pain like the deepest hell.

“For special professionals such as myself, it’s not a bad thing to erase my memories regularly. I know who I am and what my destiny is. I know that countless people are living in peace because of my sacrifice. That’s enough for me.

“Major…”

Li Yao had no idea how he was going to convince such a man.

It did make sense now that he thought about it. The two negative examples in front of Chu Zhixiao were neither the methods of the puppet king nor those of the Imperium of True Human Beings. Compared to the dark and evil forces, the ‘peace, harmony, and order’ of the Covenant Alliance was not that bad!

He had to ignite the fire of hope in his heart with a brighter, more brilliant, and more beautiful dream nation—the nation of Cultivators!

“If only there was a country where everyone was free and happy without demons and evil spirits.

Li Yao said on purpose, “If you could live in such a country without so many terrible responsibilities, you would be happier than right now!”

Chu Zhixiao smiled and made a gesture. She activated her crystal processor and drew a complicated rune in the air before she retrieved a new document and inputted it into the mainframe crystal processor of the spider tank.

“Such an ideal nation does exist. It is the nation of Cultivators.”

Chu Zhixiao said in a low voice, “However, such a beautiful country can only exist in one’s dreams. If it is implemented, disasters will only occur on a larger scale.

‘Country of Cultivators’ chilled Li Yao’s soul.

He had thought that his identity had been exposed, or that the Star Glory Federation had been discovered.

However, after reading the files sent by Chu Zhixiao carefully, he discovered that the ‘country of Cultivators’ that Chu Zhixiao mentioned had nothing to do with the Star Glory Federation at all.

The document recorded a project named ‘Eternal Brightness’.

It was… something that Li Yao could not describe. He could only call it ‘human experiment.

The Holy Alliance was more inclined to the ‘civilization of starships’ than the imperium or the federation. Many ignorant ‘soldiers’ and ‘worker bees’ lived on one starship for the rest of their lives. The sealed starship was their entire world. As long as their memories were erased from the first generation

and false information was sent in from the outside, they could be fooled into whatever environment and history they wanted.

Therefore, other than warships, carriers, and mining starships, there were almost a hundred such unique ‘experimental ships’ in the fleet of the Covenant Alliance. They had been sailing in the sea of stars for hundreds of years and set various ‘initial conditions’ to observe how the human beings on board

evolved their tiny ‘starship civilization’.

For example, some of the experiment starships would guide them to the edge of the Pangu Universe where there were no human beings or resources. They would observe what the people on the starships would do when they had no other options.

There were also some experimental starships that maintained 50% of the minimum resources and conducted research on the living conditions of human beings when resources were scarce.

For some other starships, the resources were supplied normally, but the ratio of crew members was 100 to 1, or 1 to 100. They were testing the evolution of human society in cases of extreme gender imbalance.

Another very interesting experiment was ‘Single Armageddon Warship’. All the crew members who boarded the starship were captives of the Star Ocean Imperium. After they were brainwashed, they believed that the Imperium of True Human Beings had been completely crushed by the Covenant Alliance

and that the Covenant Alliance had occupied the entire Pangu Universe. They were the ‘last hope of the civilization of mankind’.

The ‘Last Hope’ was drifting in the sea of stars. Occasionally, it would arrange some starships of the Covenant Alliance for them to ‘crush’ so that they would have enough resources to survive.

The ‘Lone Armageddon Warship’ project had been carried out for more than 370 years. The first batch of brainwashed captives of the Star Ocean Imperium had been killed a long time ago. Right now, most of the crew members were fourth-generation or fifth-generation successors. They had no doubt that

they were the ‘last members of the Star Ocean Imperium’ and that they were resisting tenaciously for the dignity of the civilization of mankind. As a result, countless soul-stirring and bizarre stories were told, which were then translated into a lot of complicated experimental data and transmitted to the

brains of the five great sages through the Fuxi System. As for their purpose, Li Yao had no idea.

The ‘Eternal Brightness’ project was similar to the ‘Single Armageddon Warship’ project.

‘When the first batch of brainwashed experiment subjects boarded the super-large starship ‘Eternal Brightness’, they were told that they came from a bright and beautiful nation of Cultivators. All the habitable planets outside were annihilated in a devastating war. Even the boundless sea of stars was filled

with all kinds of fatal radiation and storms. Only the interior of ‘Eternal Brightness’ was safe. Besides, their ancestors had brought a lot of resources and recycling facilities when they boarded the starship, which gave them endless resources.

Under the nourishment of inexhaustible and inexhaustible resources, ‘Eternal Brightness’ tumed into a perfect paradise. Everyone on board did not need to worry about survival. Everyone was fostered with a positive and righteous personality. Naturally, many cultivators were born with pure hearts and no

flaws. The great path of cultivation was the mainstream of society. There was barely any division of status and class. There were also very few contradictions and struggles. Everyone lived happily together, as if they were living in… an illusory dream.

The purpose of the experiment was probably to find out how many resources it would consume to maintain a nation of Cultivators that was perfect in theory, and whether or not such a beautiful nation of Cultivators had the ability to perfect itself and dig out the potential of human beings..

Chapter 2802: : Eternal Brightness

“The carefree country you talked about does exist. It is on ‘Eternal Brightness.

Chu Zhixiao said to Li Yao, “The environment there is beautiful, the space is spacious, and there is never the worry of lack of resources. There is no scourge of extraterrestrial devils, either. People can do whatever they want. Nobody needs to lie in wait in the frontline like the ‘soldiers’, and nobody needs to

work endlessly in the dark and damp mines like the ‘workers. All the food and energy that they need comes from a giant machine known as a ‘cyclic machine’. All the residents of ‘Eternal Brightness’ believe that it is an inexhaustible existence like a ‘perpetual motion machine’. They have never doubted the

truth of the ‘cyclic machine’, and they have no idea that the long-term ‘feeding’ of the Star Alliance is behind the ‘cyclic machine’.

“Of course, living in such a world is bliss. There is no pain at all. You can enjoy the goodness and beauty of human nature to your heart’s content. But what’s the price? The price is that, compared to normal starships of the same size, Eternal Brightness can only carry one tenth of the crew, and its food and

energy consumption are five times higher than those of starships of the same type. In other words, the resources that are enough to sustain dozens of starships and dozens of times the minimum population will be supplied to Eternal Brightness in order to maintain the ‘illusory goodness’.

“How could it be possible for the residents of Eternal Brightness to live the lives of everyone in the Sanctuary, if not the entire universe?

“More importantly, if we invest so many resources and find a way to improve ourselves in the illusionary brightness, the resources will be worth it. But the experiment of Eternal Brightness proved otherwise.

“If the first generation of residents on Eternal Brightness hundreds of years ago had not forgotten their dignity and destiny as the ‘last human beings’, and were still studying and developing in the fields of technology and Cultivation, trying to eliminate the venomous fog, pollution, and radiation on the

planets one day so that they could be revived and the civilization of mankind could be expanded again, as the old residents died and new residents were born, and the generations of residents were indulged in the love of the ‘cyclic machines’, they gradually lost their ambition and motivation and indulged

in the petty things and the hilarious hypocrisy.

“They became more and more indifferent to the outside world, and they became more and more accustomed to everything on Eternal Brightness. They were even more insatiable and demanded higher-level enjoyment.

“In the beginning, the first generation of residents could tolerate a living area of 10 square meters per person, as well as synthetic food for five days a week plus natural food for two days. Moreover, they were willing to spend more than half a day studying and working, exploring technology and

techniques of a higher level. But hundreds of years later, their extravagant children and grandchildren demanded a living area of at least 50 square meters per person, at least two delicious natural food meals per day, and a huge amount of nutritious synthetic food. Just because the taste was bland, they

didn’t care and wasted it!

“At that time, their highly-degraded brains were all immersed in meaningless entertainment. They had long forgotten the importance of studying and working. They were even unable to think of such a simple question—the space and resources on board were limited. No matter how they cycled, they

could only maintain the same degree as before. How could they create so much space and resources for them to enjoy?

“Do you know what the most important issue on Eternal Brightness right now is? The parasites who call themselves Cultivators and citizens of the Cultivation civilization are discussing whether or not those who consider themselves to be males but think that they are females are qualified to enter the

women’s restrooms and whether or not pets should be legal residents of Eternal Brightness and enjoy the same rights as them. No matter how many cats and dogs there are, they cannot be killed casually!

“Hehe. Isn’t it funny? The world outside is in flames of war. Demons and devils are everywhere. The parasites living in their sweet dreams are still concerned about men going to the ladies’ room and their pets! Such a ‘nation of Cultivators’ is so ‘beautiful’, but it is also so illusory and fragile! As long as the

Supreme Master decides to stop sending energy and food to Eternal Brightness today, the ideal nation will collapse at midnight tomorrow. It will turn from a paradise of truth and beauty into a living hell. The parasites will all turn into corpses within three days, and their corpses will all be devoured by

their pets within ten days.

“Tm giving you this example because I want to tell you that an ideal country that looks beautiful does not exist. Human beings are an imperfect creation. We need the restriction of order and the stimulation of pain all the time. Without order, our desire will be magnified infinitely and swallow us and the

entire universe. Without the stimulation of pain, we will be unable to move forward and will be reduced from the machines that transform the universe to the shameless parasites.

“know how much pain I’ve endured, and I know how many brutal battles I’ve been through. But that is exactly the purpose of my existence. I would rather live my life as ‘Slaughter Goddess’, ‘Captain of the Yaksha Team’, and ‘Major’ than live on Eternal Brightness as a fat, ignorant parasite!

While he was talking, he patted the head of the spider tank hard, as if he were trying to record his words into the crystal processor.

She had done it.

Li Yao’s head was dizzy because of Chu Zhixiao’s words, and he was dumbfounded by the truth of such a large-scale ‘lab of human nature’.

Spending the lives of hundreds of starships and generations of human beings on such a large-scale ‘human experiment’ was already beyond the limits of ‘evil’. Li Yao did not know what to say.

The brainwashing that was aimed at Chu Zhixiao was not like what Li Yao had imagined in the past, where her brain was forcibly cut off and twisted, but ‘reasonable’ and ‘reasonable’, which made her more than willing.

Scary. Too scary!

“Wouldn’t such an experiment consume a lot of resources?

Li Yao could not help but ask, “For what purpose?”

“In order to better grasp human nature, we have come up with the next development strategy of the Holy Alliance.

“It would be irresponsible of us not to conduct small-scale experiments to determine the effectiveness of our strategy, wouldn’t it?”

Li Yao was speechless. He thought for a long time and said, “However, such an experiment is false after all. The residents of Eternal Brightness did not know the truth of the outside world. That’s why they retreated and continued regressing, What if they knew part of the truth and established certain

ideals? Maybe they would have continued developing their civilization with sufficient resources?

“Even if their civilization can be developed, so what?

“Even if the residents of Eternal Brightness can break their shell, we are still unable to break the shell that covers our universe. We are all trapped in a shell, a starship that is unimaginably large and magnificent. But no matter how enormous the ‘cosmic starship’ is, the space and resources are still limited.

We still need to suppress our emotions, desires, and free will in order to survive under the protection of absolute order. Any impulsive action that seems to be blood-boiling will only accelerate our death.

“IH still don’t understand.”

Li Yao asked in fear, “Why can’t we break the shell of the ‘space starship’? Maybe there is endless space, resources, and hope outside?”

“Tt can’t be broken.”

“Hundreds of thousands of years ago,” Chu Zhixiao said casually, “the gods tried it a long time ago. Even if the shell was broken, there would only be a hundred times darker truth and more brutal enemies outside.

Li Yao was surprised. “Major, how did you know?”

“Master Zhi Shan told us.”

“Master Zhi Shan said that the Pangu Civilization Alliance, the gods hundreds of thousands of years ago, perished and hibernated to fight against the most brutal enemies outside of the universe, thereby preserving our peace for hundreds of thousands of years. The enemies even feed on the emotions and

desires of human beings. Once they smell the scent of our emotions and desires, they will invade and annihilate us. It is one of the main reasons why we have to suppress our emotions and desires.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. “Is the enemy outside the universe extraterrestrial devils?”

“No. The extraterrestrial devils are nothing compared to them.

Chu Zhixiao said, “Extraterrestrial devils are the enemies that we can understand and fight against. But the real enemies outside the universe are beyond our understanding and our capability to fight against them. We can only hide in the darkness, holding our breath, covering our mouth, and minimizing

our heartbeat and pulse. Don’t think or do anything. Wait. Wait quietly.”

Li Yao was silent for a moment. Then he asked, “What are we waiting for?”

“Waiting for the gods to wake up.

Chu Zhixiao said, “Master Zhi Shan told us that the gods are thinking of strategies to deal with the enemies beyond the universe while they are asleep, and they will certainly come up with something, All we need to do is wait patiently for the gods to wake up and provide them with sufficient resources so

that they can build themselves into the toughest army. By then, we just need to obey the commands of the gods. The day is not far away.”

“Ah”

Li Yao tried to hold back his curiosity for a long time, but he could not help but ask, “Major, do you believe Master Zhi Shan’s theory?”

Chu Zhixiao stared at Li Yao’s crystal camera in a daze until Li Yao felt scared. Then he lit another cigarette.

“Ido.”

The woman carrying the destiny of ‘Slaughter Goddess’ said gloomily, “What choice do we have except to believe in the gods?”

Chapter 2803: The Fourth Virus!

The first day of treatment had consumed too much energy for training and meetings. Even an expert such as Chu Zhixiao had fallen asleep in the deep sleep capsule.

But Li Yao was not calm for a long time.

The social status of the Holy Accord Alliance was truly an eye-opener for him. Every time, every time he thought that he had recognized the limits of the evilness of the Covenant Alliance, the fragile ‘limits’ would be broken and replaced by even more evilness, darkness, and indescribable colors.

Compared to ordinary brainwashing, everything in the Holy Light Academy was naturally evil.

But compared to the laboratories of human nature, the ‘Lone Armageddon Warship Project’, the ‘Eternal Brightness Project’, and the ‘Devil’s Child Project’ of the Holy Light Academy were nothing.

More importantly, Li Yao did not know what the five supreme masters were up to. The resources of the Star Glory Federation were not abundant in the first place, and they had wasted a lot of manpower and resources to build hundreds of experiment starships and test human nature for hundreds of years.

Why?

Li Yao vaguely felt that the ‘lab of human nature’ was the key to hiding behind the scenes of the Covenant Alliance.

As long as he could unveil the mystery of ‘why so many laboratories of human nature’, he would be able to figure out the background of the five great masters and what they were up to.

On second thought, Li Yao thought of the puppet king and his various mental drugs and brain viruses.

Wasn’t what the puppet king did another ‘human experiment’?

Did this guy know of the existence of the ‘humanity laboratory’? Did he want to use his own actions to unearth the secrets behind the ‘humanity laboratory’?

“I’ve discovered something very interesting.

While Li Yao was deep in thought, the mental devil suddenly said, “I found some data scraps deep inside the memory chip of the spider tank. After they were restored, I discovered that the conversation just now had been repeated multiple times.

“Chu Zhixiao often carried out the most dangerous missions together with the Spider Tank, and the Spider Tank did not boast as high a defense ability as hers did. Therefore, seven to eight times out of ten missions, the Spider Tank would fall apart, the lost data would retum to the blank state, and she

would have to modify it again.

“Almost every time, the spider tank would ask her the same question, and she would always try to convince the spider tank with the example of ‘Eternal Brightness’. Therefore, your test did not raise her suspicion.

“Tsee.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and asked, “Why did she explain everything to a lifeless, cold machine?”

“Idiot. The mainframe crystal processor of the spider tank is different from that of Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the Fist King. It is merely ‘appearing’ to be intelligent. In fact, the interface and the thinking database of the spider tank have been set up in advance. In other words, Chu Zhixiao wants the spider

tank to throw out the question time and time again. That’s why the spider tank is doing this.

“Don’t you see?” the mental devil said. “Didn’t I say that ‘Red Pig’, the spider tank, is a projection of the other side of Chu Zhixiao’s personality? She was not answering the question of the spider tank but the confusion in her heart. She was trying to convince herself that the pain she suffered and the twisted

order of the Covenant Alliance were necessary.

“Which means—

Li Yao suddenly realized what was going on. “This is self-deception on a certain level, isn’t it?”

“That’s right. If she really believed in her own pain and the order of the Covenant Alliance, there was no need for her to tell herself time and time again. Just like a man would not tell himself ‘you are a man’ solemnly in front of a mirror every morning, she would know it when she was peeing!

“The more guilty you are, the more suspicious you will be, and the more you will waver,” the mental devil said. “That’s why you have to fool yourself and hypnotize yourself time and time again.

“So, behind her seemingly cold and determined appearance, there is actually some normal humanity?”

Li Yao looked at the woman in the sleep capsule, who was still frowning in the soothing drugs, and made up his mind. “We can’t just believe in the Great Master and the gods. There are many things that we can believe in. We can believe in hope, in the future, and in… ourselves.

“Twill wake you up and tum you into a normal person!”

“You don’t need to be so judgmental. You can say that he is abnormal casually, but you don’t know that in the eyes of many people, you are the most abnormal one!”

The mental devil continued. “The experiments on ‘Eternal Brightness’ were indeed biased. But considering the Star Glory Federation as a model of the Cultivation world, it is not necessarily universal. Think about it. The Star Glory Federation became what it is today because of you. If you, the ‘cosmic virus’,

hadn’t transmigrated to the federation in the first place, the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Blood Demon Sector would’ve been destroyed in the war of mutual destruction. The extraterrestrial devils of the Flying Star Sector might’ve still been on a rampage, the Heaven’s Ring would’ve still been under the

repressive reign of the ‘Heavenly Dipper Clan’, the Ancient Saint World would’ve still been struggling in the seal, and eventually, all the worlds at the edge of the sea of stars would’ve been drowned by the Black Wind Fleet.

“Such a war-torn, hopeless nation of Cultivators does not seem to be as attractive as the current order and system of the Covenant Alliance!

“Maybe you are right, but am I not here?”

Li Yao’s soul was as firm as a frozen ocean. “If I can change the federation and the imperium, I will certainly change the Holy Alliance and the entire Pangu Universe. Even if I am just a furiously burning butterfly, I will always be able to illuminate a small area of brightness wherever I go. Don’t think too

much about the messy and complicated stuff. After all, I will live and die. It’s as simple as that!”

“Can I give you an opinion?”

The mental devil was silent for a moment.

“What do you want now?”

Li Yao raised his vigilance.

“It’s nothing. I admire your hot-bloodedness and courage, as well as your fearlessness as a Cultivator. But next time, can you stop comparing yourself to a butterfly?”

The mental devil replied, “You know, as a man, it feels… weird to compare yourself to butterflies all the time.

“Huh?”

“Also, don’t say ‘hey’. Are you so engrossed in your role that you are talking to me like a primary school student?

Li Yao sighed.

Chu Zhixiao was woken up by the urgent alarms after only two and a half hours of sleep. At this moment, Li Yao was crawling around her anxiously, consolidating and analyzing the tremendous data that had just been transmitted from the mainframe crystal processor of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau.

The puppet king appeared again.

Besides, this time, he had released a brand-new brain virus, one that was entirely different from the ‘Disillusionment Virus’, ‘Glory Virus’, and ‘Taotie Virus’!

Damn it.

Did the guy not need to eat, sleep, or rest? He had just engaged with the yaksha squad at a close distance three to four days ago and detonated one of the main warships of the Star Glory Federation. Now, he was planning a new round of rampant attacks!

However, it did make sense. The time left for the puppet king was the same as the time left for Li Yao.

‘Whatever schemes and plans they had, they had to be implemented before the strategical final war between the Star Glory Federation and the imperium. At the very least, they had to make the final preparations before the war.

If the army of the Star Ocean Imperium defeated the main force of the Star Ocean Imperium, the God’s Heart Union, which could mobilize tremendous computational ability, resources, and soldiers to besiege the puppet king, would be doomed even if the puppet king was ten times better at producing

viruses!

Everybody in the Heresy Interrogation Bureau understood the urgency of the situation and gave the yaksha squad the green light. After only an hour, the yaksha squad broke through the barrier of the four-dimensional space on a super-fast shuttle with the best equipment and jumped to a certain space

zone in the center of the Covenant Alliance.

‘The moment Li Yao passed through the four-dimensional space, he hacked into the mainframe crystal processor of the shuttle and read the destination of the space jump, only to learn that they had arrived at a place named ‘Gold Lion Sector’.

This place was the rear of the Covenant Alliance and also the edge of the sea of stars where resources were scarce. It was one of the few worlds that boasted precious metal resources. Although there were not many habitable planets, it was a treasure trove of special metals. The resources here were

indispensable for the refinement of armor-piercing bullets, crystal suits, and the strengthened shells of the Giant God Soldiers and starships.

More importantly, Li Yao had seen the files of the Sector in ‘Wu Yingqi 3.0.

The Golden Lion Sector was one of the few Sectors that Wu Yingqi snuck to the edge of the sea of stars with the mission of the Blood God more than a thousand years ago, trying to activate the ‘manufacturing factory’.

In other words, a manufacturing factory from hundreds of thousands of years ago was left on a certain planet or a certain star fortress. The first batch of people of the Star Glory Federation were born in this place and eventually lost control.

Li Yao estimated that the place should be a ‘recruit manufacturing and training base’ or a ‘arms factory’ of the Star Glory Federation.

It was indeed so.

Hardly had he arrived at the Golden Lion Sector when he saw a magnificent, magnificent scene through the crystal cameras of the shuttle.

A resource planet of medium size had almost been hollowed by the ant-like mining ships, revealing a giant dent that was visible to the naked eye, like a lead-gray apple that had been bitten hard.

The excavated ores, on the other hand, were absorbed into the star fortresses nearby like hundreds of gray rivers. After being smelted and cast, they would be transformed into indestructible armor-piercing bullets or reactive plate armor. Then, they would be transported to the frontline through

countless carriers, where thousands of main warships of the Holy Alliance would be armed to the teeth in order to create slaughter, destruction, and ‘eternal tranquility’.

This was not the first resource planet that had been emptied.

As far as one’s eyes could see, countless planets had been riddled with holes. They were struggling in orbit like rotten apples. Some of them even crashed into each other and turned into stone belts, like the tombs of planets.

This was not the first time that Li Yao had seen the preparation process of a great war in space.

But it was also the first time that he realized the devastating consequences of a brutal collision between such enormous beasts as the Star Glory Federation and the imperium!

Before the war began, a Sector with relatively abundant resources had already been ‘squeezed literally. What would happen to the Pangu Universe and the civilization of mankind if a strategical war was waged and every last drop of blood was shed?

Chapter 2804: Supreme Love Virus

Since it was reactivated a thousand years ago, the manufacturing factories of the Golden Lion Sector had been running day and night. Countless pure-blood, resolute ‘innate seeds’ had been ‘manufactured’, which constituted the main body of the benevolent race of the Covenant Alliance.

However, with the passage of time, the high-intensity usage and wear of magical equipment components, the production efficiency of the manufacturing plant, and the failure rate of the manufacturing plant, the cost of producing every ‘innate seed’ gradually increased to an unbearable level.

Therefore, over the past hundred years, the manufacturing factories here had gradually stagnated. Their output was only one tenth of their peak.

However, it did not mean that the status of the Golden Lion Sector had declined among the Sectors of the Star Glory Federation. A primeval relic that was compatible with the manufacturing factory was left in the place. It boasted another very important facility that kept the place as the ‘recruit training

base’ in the rear of the Star Glory Federation.

It was the ‘Meditation Temple’ that was designed to brainwash the people of the Star Glory Federation, trigger their primeval genes and cells, and awaken their new techniques.

Back in the primeval era, the Psychic Temple had been an indispensable public facility for the Pangu Civilization Alliance.

It was exactly the ‘telepathic magnetic field’ provided by the Psychic Temple that allowed the intelligent creatures of different species to understand each other’s ideology and transmit the commands of the leadership to the nerve endings of the federation.

To this day, the shrines all over the Covenant Alliance and even on the starships were the most important tools for the super experts to control the bottom-level ‘soldiers’ and ‘workers’.

The temple of the Golden Lion Sector was not only the oldest and the largest in history but also the most efficient and reliable for brainwashing.

The soldiers and civilians of the Star Ocean Imperium who had just been captured on the frontline, or the ‘soldier bees’ and the ‘worker bees’ who were born and raised in the Star Glory Federation but seemed to have their beliefs shaken, would all be sent to this place for ‘deep purification’, where their

emotions, desires, and personalities would be wiped out. Then, the brilliance of the gods and the command of their master would be poured into their blank brains, and a new, qualified member of the Star Glory Federation would be born!

Chapter 2806: Slaughterhouse Of Humanity

Li Yao gazed into the distance and scanned the appearance of the Psychic Temple with the monitoring crystal cameras on the shell of the shuttle, his own vitality, and his soul.

In the blink of an eye, he fell into the enormous, vast, deep, and unpredictable force field created by the Psychic Temple.

The body of the temple was an asteroid floating in the endless sea of stars, which had been hollowed millions of years ago. Complicated honeycombs were everywhere inside the temple. Judging from the appearance, it had been shaped into the shape of a human head.

No. Not a head, but the head of another intelligent creature. The head of a devil!

It had tentacles and long hair that looked like vipers. Dense sucking discs were growing on its thick hair, which was dancing crazily in the vacuum.

Against the long hair was a pair of eyes that almost occupied half of the face. Even the pupils were carved out vividly. The pupils were divided into nine levels, like interconnected caves leading to another mysterious universe.

In the middle of the two enormous eyes was a weird bump that looked both like a rune on his forehead and a third eye that had not been opened yet. Although the third eye had not been opened yet, Li Yao was still under tremendous mental pressure, as if the third eye would pierce through his disguise,

It did not have ears. Where a human being’s ears should be, it had grown two clusters of organs that looked both like stamens and like flames. brainwaves. The stamens were the organs that received the brainwaves.

Below the face, it had a mouth like that of human beings. It seemed that the soul of a human being had to absorb energy from food no matter how strong it was. round. It was like a super crusher that could tear apart any material, including steel.

All the features combined gave people an indescribable sense of horror. Even though Li Yao knew that it was fake, he still couldn’t help but shake his soul and his belief. In a trance, he felt that an enormous star fortress was really the head of a certain primeval devil.

as a unique training room such as the ‘Gold Crystal Tower.

No. How can there be monsters whose Cultivation speed is higher than mine in the Pangu Universe? First, it was Li Jialing, and now it’s Lu Qingchen. How can the juniors nowadays be so lawless?”

Federation. Why would a third force show up and help him? Is the so-called ‘senior expert’ from heaven?”

Li Yao could not figure out the question yet.

“Let’s turn our attention back to this guy, the guy who was talking to Gao Huan.”

Chapter 2811: Love Out Of Control

Li Yao and the mental devil grabbed at the sand deep inside the ocean of data again.

It seems to be one of the three guys.

some traces!”

“AR”

In the blink of an eye, Li Yao’s soul became incomparably firm and sharp.

smoke!

Then there was no way he could run away. There must be something wrong with this guy!

Chapter 2812: : Quick Track

The priest was just the puppet king’s tool of crime, a lizard’s tail that could be cut off at any time. He had to sneak in and capture the puppet king before the puppet king got rid of him!

The mainframe crystal processor of the spider tank lit up with an invisible and unstoppable lightning that crawled into the brain of the priest through the ubiquitous Spiritual Nexus.

of the temple through crystal cables and antennas and projected into the depths of the sea of stars.

Li Yao had never controlled his telepathic thoughts so carefully before.

The feeling of his soul being stretched to a billion kilometers, a million lightyears, and endless worlds was similar to ‘dancing’, but a hundred times more thrilling.

Chapter 2813: The Puppet King’S Low-Level Mistake

Not just the yaksha squad, even Li Yao was slightly dazed when he heard the answer.

What a coincidence. Wasn’t Eternal Brightness one of the ‘laboratories of human nature’ that Chu Zhixiao had talked to him about the day before? It was faked as a miniature nation of Cultivators and designed to test the evolution of society when human beings had abundant resources and were

inexhaustible?

He did not know that the puppet king was hiding there.

However, on second thought, it was quite normal.

Right now, the main task of the Holy Accord Alliance was to fight against the Imperium of True Human Beings. Most of the computational ability of the Great Sage had to be deployed to the battlefield, and the ‘lab of human nature’ was just a frontier exploration and a piece of idle chess. Before the

imperium was completely destroyed, the Great Sage would not pay much attention to it. Hiding there was much safer.

On the other hand, most of the residents on Eternal Brightness did not know the truth of the outside world and were isolated from the world. Their social form and the environment of the Spiritual Nexus were self-contained, which made it easier for the puppet king to lurk and manipulate them.

More importantly, if the puppet king was Lu Qingchen, the ‘nation of Cultivators’ on Eternal Brightness might remind him of his hometown, the Star Glory Federation. Maybe, it was also one of the reasons why he chose the ‘experimental warship of human nature’ as his nest.

With Guan Qixing laying the groundwork, Li Yao generously revealed the address he had locked onto.

The address that the two of them traced was almost the same, except that Li Yao was more precise. He was almost as accurate as one of the crystal processors on Eternal Brightness.

Of course, the precision was not very important, because the puppet king was not stupid enough to launch an invasion with only one crystal processor. After a large-scale operation, it would certainly destroy the evidence and replace it with a new crystal processor that had never been exposed.

Allin all, as long as the puppet king was hiding on Eternal Brightness, everything would be easier.

As it tumed out, Chu Zhixiao did not doubt that Li Yao’s ‘performance’ had suddenly improved. He only complimented that the tracking mode developed by Guan Qixing was quite useful and sent a lot of data back to the Heresy Interrogation Bureau through the communication room of the Psychic Temple.

Half an hour later, the yaksha squad had just finished resting and filling enough food and ammunition for themselves and their magical equipment when a new command was sent over.

The large-scale riot inside the Psychic Temple proved that the puppet king had grown suspicious. There was no time to lose. If he ran away again, the yaksha squad would set off immediately to destroy the Heart of God and capture the puppet king!

The difficulty of the operation was not whether or not they could destroy God’s Heart Association, but rather, they had to block every route of escape before the puppet king noticed them.

It was very likely that the puppet king had been lurking on the main force warships and the most important star fortresses of the Star Glory Federation. He would definitely notice the large-scale mobilization.

Besides, the strategical war between the Star Glory Federation and the imperium was about to begin. At such a delicate moment, it was impossible for the puppet king to disrupt all the arrangements on the frontline.

Therefore, the yaksha squad was still the main force to capture the puppet king. Of course, most of the teams of purifiers and cleaners under the command of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, as well as the special forces of the ‘God Bless’ that were adept at boarding battles, would jump to the vicinity of

Eternal Brightness to support the yaksha squad.

In the end, if possible, the five supreme masters did not want the capture operation to disrupt the normal order of Eternal Brightness, because the experiment on human nature on a large scale had lasted for three hundred years and yielded a lot of interesting data. The higher-ups were hoping that the

ignorant world could continue developing and see how far the human desire could grow and how much it could corrupt the spirituality of human beings.

If they were consumed by the flames of war, all their previous endeavors would be wasted. It would take them another three hundred years to accumulate enough resources.

Of course, more importantly, the life and death of the puppet king had to be captured alive. He could not run away, nor could he commit suicide in fear of punishment, because the higher-ups had the same idea as Li Yao. They all believed that the puppet king could not have appeared out of thin air. Maybe

the puppet king was a puppet of a mysterious existence, and the ‘mysterious existence’ was the most dangerous enemy of the Covenant Alliance!

The code name of the operation was ‘Ghost Catching’.

An hour later, the ghost-catching operation officially began.

Carrying such an arduous mission, all the members of the yaksha squad, together with a team of elite cleaners and the elite special forces selected from the ‘God Bless Army’, boarded a tusk-shaped assault starship and slowly sailed into the most suitable space for space jumps at the edge of the Golden

Lion Sector.

Sensing that the vast sea of stars outside the cabin window was turning more and more illusionary, Li Yao suddenly had a weird feeling.

Fast. It was too fast.

It had only been a few days since he learned of the existence of the puppet king, when he snuck into the yaksha squad in the form of a spider tank and participated in the mission to capture the puppet king, when he jumped from the Heresy Interrogation Bureau to the Psychic Temple, and when he was

about to jump from the Psychic Temple to Eternal Brightness.

The missions in the Covenant Alliance were completely different from those in the federation and the imperium.

There was no need to mention the Star Glory Federation. Even in the Imperium of True Human Beings, the people he was faced with were living, breathing, and full of personality.

Li Linghai, the empress of the Star Ocean Imperium; Dong Fangwang, the prime minister of the imperium; Jin Yuyan, the manager of the General Commerce Alliance; and even Wu Yinggi, the Black Star Emperor. They were all specific people with their own beliefs, ambitions, and desires.

However, in the Covenant Alliance, except for the eccentric creatures such as ‘Devil’s Child’ and ‘Yaksha’, the lowest-level ‘soldier bees’ and ‘worker bees’, the priests of the ‘best’ race, the director of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, and even the ‘best’ master who had just appeared in the sacred light of the

crystal processor, all of them were featureless, had no personality, and had fuzzy faces. They did not look like specific human beings at all. Instead, they were symbols, symbols, and components on a giant machine.

Imagine an enormous, precise, and solid machine made of billions of components the size of a planet. Everybody, including the chief of the inquisition and the supreme master, would be insignificant screws that could be replaced at any time inside the machine. It was exactly how Li Yao felt when he was

carrying out a mission.

With a starship as the carrier, one would be drifting in the vast sea of stars forever, making one feel insignificant and lonely.

A starship, on the other hand, was not enough to maintain the most fundamental circulation of resources. It had to rely on the intangible Spiritual Nexus to receive missions and supplies from an unknown place. The belief and admiration for a certain supreme force were deepened, too. Without anybody

knowing it, its personality and self had been deprived, and it had turned into a small part of the enormous machine.

So much so that, as long as a random command was sent from the portable crystal processor, it would become a law. It was enough for people to contribute and sacrifice without any hesitation. There was simply no need to figure out who sent the command, nor was there any need to think about the truth

behind the command.

It was a literal, out-and-out ‘national machine’ that had been fully activated.

However

‘There was always a manipulator behind any machine.

Even if there weren’t, in the past, there would always be someone who would set the procedures and tasks of the machine.

‘Who was the controller of the enormous machine that was the Covenant Alliance? The five fuzzy, personalityless masters, the sleeping Pangu Clan, or… something else?

While Li Yao was pondering, he sensed the soul ripples from the mental devil.

“Hey, do you feel that something is not right?”

The mental devil.

“What’s weird?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment and asked back.

“It is exactly why the puppet king is so easy to locate.

“Think about it,” the mental devil said. “The puppet king shattered all the surveillance videos and data when he activated Gao Huan. He deleted them time and time again, and it was almost impossible to recover them. If we were not talented and his old friends who were familiar with his methods, it would

have been impossible to recover them.

“It is evident that he does not want his existence to be discovered, right?

“Isn’t that obvious?”

Li Yao replied, “Of course, the puppet king does not want other people to find him. Finding him means death. Is there a problem?”

“Don’t be hasty. Listen to me.”

“The problem is,” the mental devil said, “if the puppet king did not want anybody to find him, why would he keep the high priest under his control alive?

“Well…”

Li Yao thought quickly and immediately realized what was going on. Having been enlightened by the mental devil, he realized that something was not right, too. “It does make sense. On the surface, the fierce battle in the Psychic Temple just now was truly soul-stirring. A lot of infected people were

raging, their flesh and blood flying everywhere. A high-level priest fled in a hurry and self-destructed in the end. It was also a special skill that we played on the Spiritual Nexus. Our telepathic thoughts were flying randomly between almost ten Sectors, making it look like a thrilling movie.

“However, there was no need to go through so much trouble. There was no need for the puppet king to keep the priest alive in the first place. After he completed the task of ‘activating Gao Huan’, he could’ve killed the priest by silencing him. Then, everything would’ve been settled and nobody would’ve

known that he was hiding on Eternal Brightness.

“Alright. Let’s say that the high priest was an important chess piece that he arranged in the Psychic Temple. It was not easy for him to corrupt the high priest. If he was reluctant to kill the high priest in the beginning, he should’ve killed him and silenced him when the yaksha squad came to the Psychic

Temple for investigation.

“Chu Zhixiao and the rest of them interrogated dozens of workers before it was the high priest’s turn. The puppet king clearly had plenty of time to ‘snail off. Why didn’t he do that? Why did he have to make such a thrilling scene? In the end, we still caught his tail and locked onto his real body? Such a

low-level mistake does not seem likely to be made by the puppet king—Lu Qingchen…”

Chapter 2814: The Ghost Catching Operation Begins!

Li Yao’s soul rippled for a long time, but he still could not figure out what was going on. “Did the guy set up a trap on purpose to lure the yaksha squad to Eternal Brightness? In fact, he has already turned Eternal Brightness into a perilous battlefield?”

“But that can’t be right. The yaksha squad is just a special combat unit under the command of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau. There are not many of them. They are not the core strength of the Holy Alliance. In fact, they are the ‘experiments’ that are being monitored in the first place. Even if the yaksha

squad is entirely wiped out, they will not be able to hurt a single hair of the five great masters. There is no reason for the puppet king to go through all the trouble and use himself as bait to annihilate a mere yaksha squad, is there?

“Therefore, if everything was within his plan, he must have a deeper purpose!

“Maybe. There’s something else that I need to remind you of. Think about it carefully. What are we talking about when we are talking about the real ‘Puppet King’?”

The mental devil replied, “We both know that, if the puppet king is really Lu Qingchen, he must’ve gotten rid of his shell a few years ago and turned into a pure Heavenly Demon of the Spiritual Nexus. Or according to him—the third form of human beings after human beings and demons, the ‘Spirit Clan’!

“The body of flesh and blood that he brought out of his mother’s womb was lost in the war when the Black Wind Fleet assaulted the Star Glory Federation. It was blown to smithereens on ‘Black Swirl’, the flagship of the Black Wind Fleet. From then on, he was granted absolute freedom and was no longer

bound by any shell.

“Therefore, it is very likely that there is no ‘true self that can be captured by the yaksha squad!”

“Right!”

Li Yao said, “Lu Qingchen is an astral being without a shell. Or rather, the entire Spiritual Nexus is his shell… But then again, consciousness cannot exist without matter for a long time. Even though he is in the form of an information life-form just like us, he still needs something to rely on so that his

soul power can be fully released!

“Just like we have to use the mainframe crystal processor and the limbs of the spider tank, Lu Qingchen has to be attached to something in order to wield his strength!

“Yes. But in such a case, Lu Qingchen’s real face might be completely different from what the yaksha squad imagined.

“At this moment, the yaksha squad, the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, and the Grand Master all think that they are trying to capture someone,” the mental devil said. “But in fact, it is very likely that Lu Qingchen does not exist, or that he has more than one body of a human being. His real body might be the

mainframe crystal processor of Eternal Brightness, or a fast shuttle inside the starship city, or… anything.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment. “Damn. Why do I feel that the more we discuss, the more difficult it will be to capture the puppet king? Should we think of a way to remind Chu Zhixiao and the rest of them of the real form of the puppet king?”

“How?”

The mental devil asked back, “Are you going to tell Chu Zhixiao that the puppet king is actually not a human being but some kind of… Spirit Clan, Super Spiritual Entities, Heavenly Demons of the Spiritual Nexus? What? How do you know that? It’s simple. Since you and the puppet king are old

acquaintances, and you are his elder, you watched the puppet king grow up. You even patted the puppet king’s hair and kindly encouraged him to study hard and improve himself every day. You even saw him when he was still wet behind the ears. He even served you tea and was very respectful to you!”

“Alright.”

Li Yao surrendered. “Forget it. It seems that we can only play by ear again. Thankfully, playing by ear has always been our specialty!”

While the two of them were talking to themselves, the assault starship went through another four-dimensional jump and left the Golden Lion Sector, arriving in a new world.

It was a barren world far away from the frontline. Most of the space around it that was suitable for space jumps was wreathed in stone belts and high-energy nebulae. It was as blocked as a basin on a planet.

Other than a dying, lackluster star and a few lethargic planets, there was nothing of value here.

Eternal Brightness was stationed in the orbit of the largest planet in the world.

The people of the Star Glory Federation had lied to the ignorant residents of Eternal Brightness that their ‘recycling machine’ had absorbed endless, inexhaustible resources from the planet, and that they could live a carefree life for tens of thousands of years before the star entered its decline.

In fact, the planet with an extremely harsh environment was full of crumbly rocks. Even though it was tens of thousands of meters below the ground, there was not even the shadow of a crystal the size of a fingernail, let alone the atmosphere and the ecosystem.

Even if all the residents on Eternal Brightness were awakened, they would not be able to run away with the resources of the planet.

Li Yao had thought that the people of the Star Glory Federation were all ruthless machines.

But at the very least, the names of the Sectors had a cruel sense of humor and named the place ‘Beyond the Well’.

Wasn’t the life of the residents of Eternal Brightness like the moon in a well? No matter how splendid it was, it was just a dream. If one were to pick it up with their hands, it would immediately crumble into vague ripples.

‘When the assault starship reached the boundary of the well, a rarely-seen meteor shower was attacking Eternal Brightness, leaving colorful, fireworks-like circles on the spiritual shield of the starship.

Naturally, it was also part of the plan.

It was because the tentacles of the puppet king had probably infiltrated every corner of Eternal Brightness and infiltrated the mainframe crystal processor and the crystal cameras and sensors installed around the starship.

If they did not do anything and allowed the assault starships, hunting starships, and interference starships, including the yaksha squad and the special forces of the God Bless Army, to jump to the ‘well world’, it was very likely that the puppet king would notice them immediately. In the next second, the

puppet king would run away through the Spiritual Nexus or simply commit suicide.

Therefore, such a meteor shower was arranged so that a lot of pieces of the meteorites would bombard the shell of Eternal Brightness. Some of the larger meteorites would even pierce through the spiritual shield and the alloy shell of the starship precisely.

In such a way, the ‘eyes’ and ‘ears’ that were scattered on the shell of the starship would have to be tumed off for a while. Even the mainframe crystal processor would have to be fully repaired and examined. He believed that the puppet king would not dare hide inside during the time.

It was the best opportunity for the hunting squads of the ‘Ghost Catching Operation’ to jump to the middle of the well and draw close to Eternal Brightness.

It was also the only way to implement the ‘Spiritual Nexus Blockage’ and lock the puppet king within a square inch.

Eternal Brightness was divided into two layers. They were two zones that did not interfere with each other.

Most of the crew members who stayed in the cockpit and the outer layer of the starship were from the Covenant Alliance.

Some of them were Meditation Healers who had a deep understanding of human nature and brains, some were priests who were extremely devoted to the gods, and some of them were spies in the training. The first lesson of the spies was to fool the residents on Eternal Brightness before they were sent to

the real battlefield to be tested by the imperium.

The inner layer, on the other hand, was enveloped by the 3D light beams and the Grand Illusionary Land. The people living in the starship cities did not know where they were and only knew that they were the real subjects of the experiments.

‘The inner layer and the outer layer were usually isolated. The residents on board had been told since they were young that the bridge and the outer cabin were full of intense radiation and high-energy particle streams. They were not allowed to go out easily unless they had received the harshest training

and injected countless drugs. Otherwise, they might get incurable diseases.

After decades of dangerous education, 99% of the residents often abandoned the idea of exploring the bridge and the outer cabins and enjoyed themselves in the small paradise.

Of course, there were also a few people who, no matter how they were warned and educated, had always dreamed of becoming a qualified starship operator and seeing the real cockpit, the real thrusters, the real engine compartment, and the real weapon platform.

There was a school in the starship city that was specially prepared for such brave people.

99% of the brave ones would be eliminated in the rigorous tests. Their dreams would be cut off, and their ambitions would be wasted in the sea of stars in the Grand Illusionary Land.

Only one in ten thousand people had a slim chance to break through all the obstacles and become a glorious crew member. They would have to go to the bridge or the outer cabins and see the truth. Of course, before that, they would have to go through a minor procedure called brainwashing.

Therefore, the yaksha squad, the cleaners, and the purifiers, as well as the special forces of the God Bless Army, could not sneak in with such honor.

After three hundred years of seclusion, the features of the residents of Eternal Brightness had changed a lot from those of the outside world. Their clothes and manners were vastly different, too. If they broke into the forbidden area recklessly, a riot would definitely break out, which would be equivalent

to informing the puppet king of their arrival.

Therefore, when the assault starships, hunting starships, and interference starships approached Eternal Brightness under the cover of the meteor shower, the first thing they did was disguise themselves with the help of the staff of Eternal Brightness.

It was mainly about sticking some fat, soft man-made muscles and fats that were known as ‘biochemical muscles’ to the body.

Very soon, Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin finished their disguises and turned into two plump, red, and translucent men.

Li Yao and Guan Qixing, on the other hand, were quite simple. After all, there were spiritual puppets in the form of spiders and artificial bodies on Eternal Brightness. All they needed to do was to modify them and replace them with local-style shells.

Only the stocky monster named ‘Titan’ Yuan Kuo was more troublesome to deal with. He could not hide his height of four to five meters no matter how hard he tried. In the end, he had no choice but to curl up in a closed truck and lock his restless strength with rune arrays while he followed the squad..

Chapter 2815: A Bright Future

In the entire operation, nine hunting teams had been deployed. A lot of soldiers had been lurking in the outer cabin of Eternal Brightness, and several warships of the God Bless were lurking behind the other planets inside the well.

And the core of all this was the yaksha squad.

Therefore, as the information transmission center of the yaksha squad, Li Yao was also carrying a piece of powerful communication magical equipment on his back, which had a few long antennas that could receive and transmit astronomical information every second.

The structure of the starship city, the distribution of every complicated tube, the movements of the nine teams, the coordinates of the soldiers lurking in the periphery, and so on. All the information was analyzed and processed by Li Yao before it was transmitted to the crystal processors of the four core

members of the yaksha squad.

Ten minutes later, the nine teams finished their disguises and infiltrated the starship city through the secret channels.

The starship city on Eternal Brightness was named ‘Glorious City’. It was a circular city about five kilometers in diameter with skyscrapers everywhere. The tallest skyscrapers were almost interconnected from the two sides, like colorful bridges that cut through the sky.

The artificial gravity generated by the continuous revolutions attracted people to the curved ground. The gravity here was slightly lighter than that of the earth-like planets, making the residents of the place fair and fat, as plump and fragile as peaches. With a leap, they would be able to stop in midair

easily. Therefore, a lot of new ways of moving were born, such as jumping from the ground to the sky and back to the ground from the other side of the sky with the help of the jet propulsion while wearing a loose suit.

As a symbol of the City of Light, it was also the greatest pride and symbol for the residents of the city. It was the amusement park in the center of the city that never stopped operating.

The ever-revolving Nightless City could be said to be the epitome of human beings’ imagination of ‘happiness’. It was truly an eternal paradise.

The yaksha squad appeared from the basement of a skyscraper that was being fully maintained. Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin had put on the unique clothes that were made of gauze and silk and looked like kids playing house.

The shells of Li Yao and Guan Qixing were painted with dazzling rainbow patterns, too, making them look like two big toys.

Before they reached the center of the city, they were already assaulted by an intoxicating aroma.

The fragrance was somewhat hypnotizing and hallucinogenic. It could make people feel happy and happy. They could not help but giggle.

It was true that the world of the asura, which had been filled with smoke and killing intent, was now a peaceful and peaceful city of brightness. For the people who had been soaked in blood all the time and whose bodies and souls had been savaged, it was like a different world.

Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin looked at each other and gestured at Guan Qixing and Li Yao before they walked out of the building, somewhat alarmed.

At this moment, the enclosed carrier carrying Yuan Kou slowly drove out of the parking lot below the building.

In front of them was an ocean of joy.

Today seemed to be some sort of festival in Bright City. It seemed to be called “Rainbow Carnival’. There was no telling what it was about, but the residents of Bright City would hold similar celebrations every few days.

Some of them were celebrating the 300th anniversary of their rebirth, some were celebrating the 250th anniversary of the same-sex marriage legislation, some were celebrating the 200th anniversary of the birth of the “third gender’, some were celebrating the 100th anniversary of the “Pet Inheritance

Act”, and so on.

Or maybe, it was because the resources produced by the ‘recycling machine’ were too many and he was merely spending them for fun.

Allin all, the 3D light beams embedded in the walls all around created a lollipop-like sun, a rainbow made of cotton candy-like clouds and rainbow candies. The dazzling fireworks in the sky exploded in laughter one after another. The facades of the skyscrapers turned into giant light beams, too, showing

the jubilation of the citizens. Everybody in the city crowded into the streets and threw flowers, candies, cakes, and delicious food at each other. They painted each other into colorful cats and giggled.

For a moment, Li Yao felt a great… cultural impact.

He had never been so shocked when he was faced with the star child from the Imperium of True Human Beings, the resurrected Pangu Clan, or Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi.

Growing up in an environment where resources were extremely scarce, he never knew what ‘extremely abundant resources’ meant.

It should be known that in both the federation and the imperium, even if one had cultivated to the Nascent Soul Stage or even the Divinity Transformation Stage, regardless of whether one was a ‘good person’ or a ‘bad person’, they would cherish every bit of resources. Even if a grain of rice fell on the

table, they would stick it to their fingers and eat it—this was the lifestyle of many experts.

Although not everyone was like this, Li Yao had never seen such a scene in his life where an entire city of people were squandering and wasting without caring about the cake and food on the ground.

If he had a heart, it would’ve been twisted into a ball right now.

Li Yao’s soul was tightening like a rope while he observed everything around him.

He discovered that, by the standard of the residents of the planet, the residents of Bright City were all too fat. The men and the women, young and old, were almost all red balls in different sizes. Not just their bones, even their muscles were barely distinguishable, as if there was a pool of clear water below

their smooth skin.

Although it was true that living inside a starship for a long time in an artificial gravity environment would lead to bone loss and other problems, the residents of the Flying Star Sector, who were also part of the ‘starship civilization’, did not look as prosperous and satisfactory. The residents of the Flying

Star Sector were even slimmer and looked like light tadpoles.

It seemed that he had eaten too well and worried too little.

Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin could pretend to be residents of the City of Light with their ‘biochemical muscles’, or they could put on clothes that were as colorful and childish as theirs, but it was difficult to imitate their expressions and temperament.

How could one describe their spirit?

On the bright side, the residents of this place all had ‘unbullied faces’. They were all carefree and never thought that there were any disasters, suffering, or darkness in the world. They were all innocent and had no wariness at all.

In the worst-case scenario, they were heartless and had no ability to protect themselves or survive at all. They were all lambs waiting to be slaughtered, flowers nurtured in sterile cells.

Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin were apparently not used to the environment and the appearance of the people here, either. Despite their fat heads and big ears, wariness and cruel fighting will were still shining deep inside their eyes.

At this moment, several local residents, carrying balloons and delicious food, bounced over.

“Hey, happy Rainbow Festival!”

One of the residents suddenly threw a cake painted in rainbow colors at Chu Zhixiao.

With Chu Zhixiao’s Cultivation, it was not difficult for her to dodge the cake. However, she was not sure whether or not she would expose herself by doing so. Therefore, she could only allow the cake to smash her face. She blinked in confusion amid the flowing cream.

“Hahahaha!”

The few locals burst into laughter and grimaced. “Come on. Cheer up and fight!”

Then, they jumped up and opened their arms. Two streams of air spurted out of their boots and floated into the sky like a few giant balloons. They flew toward the glamorous amusement park while waving at Chu Zhixiao and the rest of the Cultivators, as if they were challenging them.

It seemed to be the definition of ‘battle’ in the eyes of the local residents.

“What a… terrifying place.”

The mental devil said to Li Yao in a low voice, “The residents here are very friendly, but they are also childish and empty. They seem to have nothing to do because of the abundant resources. In order to avoid the emptiness, they came up with all kinds of celebrations to pass the time. However, the

dullness after the celebrations made them sink into a vicious cycle of emptiness. For hundreds of years, they failed to make any progress in technology and exploration.

“But then again, they are birds in a cage. There is no room for exploration or improvement. What else can they do except sing and smile?

“L.. ama little scared of this place.”

Looking at the locals who were dancing and dancing in midair, Li Yao mumbled, “No. I’m not scared of this place so much as I’m scared of the future where the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Covenant Alliance are united and there is no threat from the outside. The technology

has improved a lot, but it is still not enough to break out of the black wall.

“In the current civilization of mankind, both the federation and the imperium, although there are all kinds of serious problems and even fatal threats, it is precisely because of these problems and threats that all our courage and wisdom can be stimulated, and only then can we be forced to show the

brightest and most beautiful side of human nature and mold us into… stronger and stronger human beings.

“However, if our endeavors succeed one day, and the contradiction between the Star Glory Federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance is shattered, all the human beings in the Pangu Universe will be united into an inseparable whole. There will be no more wars, no more oppression, and no more

threats from the outside. The advanced technology will also provide everyone with the most basic happiness. By then, will my fellow human beings be as carefree and naive as the residents of the City of Light? Will they be dwelling in the small, certain happiness that is right in front of their eyes?

Chapter 2816: : Erosion Of Eternal Peace

“Don’t forget the multiverse, the tides, and Earth.

“Each of the targets is enough to encourage the civilization of mankind to struggle for another ten thousand years,” the mental devil said.

“Too far. These targets are too far away.”

Li Yao said, “Even the most basic part of ‘crossing the black wall’ might not be completed in five thousand years without the intervention of external forces and the accidental explosion of technology.

“Humans are lazy. When you know that all the missions have been completed, and all the threats in front of you are no more, and you will have to struggle for at least five thousand years to see the results of the next mission, and the results may not necessarily be correct, and the next threat may still be

billions of lightyears away, so far away that even ‘lightyears’ can’t measure it, will you still rack your brain and burn everything to struggle?

“The residents of Light City were also burdened with the great mission of ‘resurrecting the civilization of mankind’. But the mission was too magnificent, too far away, and too heavy. It was so heavy that they did not know how to begin. In the end, they gradually abandoned and forgot it.

“Such is human nature. It is impossible to demand that everybody give up the happiness of the modern world and pursue the hope that can only be found after thousands of years.

“Born in trouble, die in peace; born in peace, die in trouble. It is the same for both individuals and civilizations.

After he finished his speech, the mental devil suddenly smiled. “Therefore, this is the purpose of the existence of the extraterrestrial devils, especially those who love killing and destruction like me. You were against me in the beginning. But think about it carefully. What will the civilization of mankind

become if there is no killing and destruction, no knowledge of the methods and meanings of combat?

Li Yao was dazed for a long time. He stared at the residents of the City of Light, who were as clumsy as a giant balloon and had balloons tied to their waists, while they giggled and slapped each other with cakes. Somebody even asked their pet dog to ride on his back. It was hard to tell whose pet it was.

Some of their friends and family or passers-by captured their hilarious pictures and played them on the glass walls of skyscrapers or even the 3D light beams of the cabins. Everybody commented on them and offered flowers and decorations to them as if they were very proud and happy.

For some reason, Li Yao felt cold.

“Although I tried my best to stop every war in the sea of stars and hoped that peace would be eternal, you do seem to have a point. It has never occurred to me what eternal peace will do for the civilization of mankind.

Complicated and deep ripples spread out from the depths of Li Yao’s soul. He mumbled, “I suddenly understand what the Nuwa Clan did years ago.

“It must be noted that, although we objected to the Pangu Clan’s seclusion and cowardice, the way that the Nuwa Clan released their emotions and desires to the maximum and rushed out of the Pangu Universe despite the civil war seemed a bit too radical.

“The concerns of the Pangu Clan are not entirely unfounded. The sea of stars outside the Pangu Universe is too vast, the multiverse is too dark, and the enemy behind the flood is too strong, If we act hastily without making thorough preparations, it is indeed possible that we will be killed.

“Why can’t the Nuwa Clan wait patiently for thousands of years, or even ten thousand years, until the flood ebbs and the conditions are more mature? Then, they can seize the leadership of the Pangu Civilization Alliance in a more peaceful way and start a new expedition. Isn’t that better?

“In the past, when we discussed the issue with Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, and the rest of them, we all believed that the Nuwa Clan was attracted by the voices from the outside of the Pangu Universe and the ‘undead of the civilizations that had been swallowed by the flood. They fell into a frenzy and could

not free themselves.

“But today, after seeing the mental appearance and way of life of the residents of Bright City, I suddenly had a new idea.

“Maybe, the Nuwa Clan knew that they were too rash, too radical, and too risky. Their hope was slim, and they would be doomed if they failed.

“But they had no other choice.

“The growth and continuation of a civilization has a period. The growth of a civilization is like sailing against the current. If you don’t move forward, you will fall behind. There is absolutely no reason for a civilization to develop steadily for ten thousand years.

“If the Pangu Civilization Alliance had lived in seclusion in the Pangu Universe for ten thousand years, fifty thousand years, or even a hundred thousand years in accordance with the wishes of certain people of the Pangu Clan, it would have been impossible for the Pangu Civilization Alliance to maintain

the state-of-the-art technology of a hundred thousand years ago and maintain its positive and aggressive outlook. It would have ended up like the residents of Light City, a fat, pitiful worm, an adult physically and a baby mentally!

“A civilization does not have internal and external troubles. Or rather, it will not arrive until tens of thousands of years later. It will perish sooner or later. The Nuwa Clan has come to realize that. Therefore, even though they knew that the preparation work was not completed yet, they still risked their

lives and rushed out. They even started a civil war to ‘wake up’ some of their compatriots and remind them not to forget their mission!

“That’s an interesting assumption.

“The evolution of any civilization is never done step by step,” the mental devil said. “There are many times when one has to make the most dangerous leap in the face of a bottomless abyss even though one knows that a tiger is lurking in the depths of the abyss. After the leap, the civilization will continue

and rise to a higher level. The outcome will be the same if one falls into the abyss or does not dare to jump. The only difference is that the civilization will die sooner or later.

“Yes. I thought that we would have thousands of years, if not tens of thousands of years, to develop slowly and establish an expedition army neither too fast nor too slowly. We could explore the outer domain ten thousand years later.

“But now that I think about it,” Li Yao said, “if ‘the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance resolve their conflicts, the great unity of the Pangu Universe will take a thousand years, and ‘building an expedition army and breaking through the black wall’ will take ten thousand years, how will the

civilization of mankind develop in the seemingly peaceful, unthreatened nine thousand years?”

“Will the new generation of human beings, who are carefree from the moment they are born, have the unique sense of crisis that we, the ‘old human beings’, developed in the war years?

“Can they understand plots, betrayals, bloodshed, cruelty, extinction, sacrifice, and devotion?

“Can they understand the destiny of the human civilization and why we have to rush out of the black wall and face the unpredictable dangers?

BOOM! CRACK!
