There was no telling whether it was an illusion or reality. There was no telling whether it was mental or physical. Two surging, unparalleled forces fell upon the puppet king like a mountain.

In Li Yao’s senses, they were indeed two magnificent, solemn, three-headed, six-armed statues hundreds of meters tall. Surrounded by holy light, they glanced over all the living creatures in the world with an air of control, forgiveness, and suppression.

Just a moment ago, the puppet king’s power of darkness had been intimidating and unstoppable.

But right now, suppressed by the two statues, he was like a clown who was at his wit’s end.

“Puppet King, now—”

The two statues of gods and devils roared at the same time. Their mental power was highly compressed and condensed into dazzling mental lightning again, which darted toward the center of the medical center. Every explosion of the lightning turned into an earsplitting noise. “Who is the mantis trying

to stop a chariot? Do you not know your place?”

“This—this is—”

Li Yao reacted instinctively and retreated to the deepest part of the spider tank.

Of the two statues, one seemed to be made of endless magma and flames, and the other seemed to be made of the coldest seawater in the deep sea. Together with the immense mental power, what could they be if not the appearance of the greatest master?

“Are they ‘Master Phantom Sea’ and ‘Master Sky Burning’?””

Li Yao mumbled to himself.

The five supreme masters corresponded to the five tribes of Water, Wood, Gold, Fire, and Earth’. They were the Sea Clan’s Master Phantom Sea, the Fire Clan’s Master Sky Firing, the Gold Clan’s Master Sharp Gold, the Earth Clan’s Master Earth, and the Wood Clan’s Master Deadwood.

Among them, the Water Clan was the core of the five tribes to nurture priests and commanders. ‘Master Phantom Sea’ was also the leader of the five supreme masters. His mental strength, soul strength, and understanding of belief were the most profound.

The Fire Clan was a tribe of warriors. ‘Sky Burning Master’, the leader of the Fire Clan, was naturally one of the most distinguished warriors.

Right now, in order to capture Lu Qingchen, the king of puppets, not only had the yaksha squad, the cleaners, the special forces of the God Bless Army, the ‘photon troops’, and even the two strongest masters of spiritual energy and combat ability come in person, the lineup could not have been more

splendid.

No wonder Lu Qingchen, the puppet king who had secured his victory, was dazed for a long time when his soul was caught unprepared. Then he roared in fury, “It’s impossible. It’s impossible. Hasn’t the strategical final battle between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation already begun?

Didn’t you march toward the heart of the Star Glory Federation with the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation? How did you end up in this place? It was all an illusion. You were merely bluffing. You can’t fool me!”

“What?”

Li Yao was surprised again.

Judging from what Lu Qingchen said, the final war between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation had already begun. The main force of the Star Glory Federation had already jumped to the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Planet.

It was—it was too fast. It was impossible. There was no reason for it!

“Puppet King, are you still unrepentant even though you are about to die?”

“Even though the final war between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation is about to break out, how can we go all out and destroy the Star Glory Federation without getting rid of you, the fatal threat?

“We know that your plan is to expand the strength of the Heart of God while the war between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation is heating up so that you can absorb more dark forces and produce more brain viruses that will corrupt the body of the Star Glory Federation and the

countless innocent and sacred members of the Star Glory Federation.

“Therefore, we released false news to you on purpose so that you would think that the final war between the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation had begun and that we had been killed by the experts of the Star Glory Federation and the Empyrean Terminus Sector. That’s why you were bold

enough to mess around and let us lock onto your true self!

“In fact, everything about the imperium is under our control. The real final battle will begin in three days, which will be used to refine an unpardonable bandit such as yourself.

The so-called ‘best path’ required everyone to be equal. From the lowest level ‘soldier bees’ and ‘work bees’ to the highest level ‘master of kindness’, theoretically speaking, it was just a division of labor. There was no hierarchy.

Therefore, when he opened his mouth, he simply addressed himself as ‘we’ instead of T or ‘T.

It was the opposite of the strict hierarchy of the imperium.

Li Yao was even more shocked. He recalled Yun Haixin’s explanation about the ‘real spies’ and wondered whether or not the spies of the Star Glory Federation had really infiltrated the core of the imperium and knew the military movements of the imperium like the back of their hand. Was that why they

could arrive at the final battle so precisely?

Three days. If Master Sky Firing was not lying and the final battle was only three days away, what could he do exactly?

“Haha. A brutal, unpardonable criminal?”

Deep inside the medical center, after a moment of silence, the puppet king, Lu Qingchen, burst into wild laughter again. “You are just a bunch of pretentious liars and self-degenerating puppets. What makes you think that you are qualified to accuse me of being an unpardonable criminal? Hahahaha. Let’s

not talk about ‘Phantom Ocean’ and ‘Sky Burning’. Even if the five supreme masters come at me at the same time, I will still challenge you and show you the true spirit of mankind!”

“Not good—”

Li Yao thought of something and said in a low voice, “The puppet king is running away.”

The mental devil said, “What? Isn’t he going to ‘fight’?”

“Fight my ass.”

Li Yao said solemnly, “In such a situation where the enemy is stronger than us and the situation is extremely unfavorable for us, the more we talk big, the more hot-blooded we are, and the more guilty we are, the more we are prepared to run away. Believe me, I know a lot about such cowards and bullies

the weak.

“Okay…” the mental devil replied.

As it tumed out, before the crazy laughter of the puppet king died down, before the negative emotions lingering in the sky were swept away by the two supreme masters, Li Yao and the mental devil had vaguely sensed a vague trace of darkness and snuck out of the medical center, hoping to reach the

periphery of Eternal Brightness through the Spiritual Nexus and the crystal cables all over the city.

“Puppet King, come to your senses in the light of the gods!”

The silent Master Mirage Sea finally spoke in a tranquil, gentle, and indifferent voice.

A giant hand that seemed to be made of deep seawater extended out and snatched the ground precisely in the direction of the black streak.

Not just the puppet king, even Li Yao, who had been lurking nearby, felt that the attack was aimed at him. His soul condensed rows of sharp thorns uncontrollably.

The puppet king was even squeaking in pain. Its soul, which was trying to run away, had to retreat to the depths of the medical center. It simply revealed the appearance of the dark octopus again and expanded like a balloon that had been blown up. Soon, it was the size of the two statues. The three

statues filled the entire circular city. There was not even a gap for anybody to catch their breath.

Even if one’s five senses were a bit dull, if they were in Bright City, they would still be able to sense the intense fluctuations of the magnetic field, causing them to have a heart palpitation, a splitting headache, shivering, and so on.

Those with keen senses, on the other hand, whose retinas had been affected by the magnetic field, could simply ‘see’ the fierce battle of the three Dharma Idols and feel the destructive force. Even their brains and souls were torn apart by the immense blast.

Li Yao was amazed and speechless at the same time. He watched, scanned, analyzed, and duplicated the details of the soul ripples, the magnetic field model, and the structure of the illusions.

A soul-stirring confrontation between the puppet king and the two great masters of wisdom was not something that could happen every day.

Ifhe could digest every detail of the battle, Li Yao’s understanding about soul and mental battles would definitely be much higher, and he would be able to understand more fundamental things. Of course, it was also possible that he could grasp the fatal weakness of the Puppet King, the Phantom Ocean,

and the Skyblaze!

“Like I said, our advantage is huge and getting bigger and bigger, isn’t it?

Li Yao held his head and bulged his crystal camera while he faced the mental devil.

For ultimate experts at the level of the Puppet King and the Great Master, the duration of the fierce battle could only be calculated in milliseconds. Besides, their bodies of flesh and blood were not in this place, but in their real bodies. The mental battle was even more unpredictable. The outcome of the

battle was determined within a moment.

In the time it took for Li Yao to ‘scan’ the puppet king, hundreds of black tentacles had burst out of the puppet king’s body, trying to pierce into the two masters’ bodies and corrupt the magma and the telepathic thoughts of the Nether Sea.

The two masters of wisdom did not dodge. Instead, they extended their hands and formed hundreds of giant palms in midair, which smashed at the puppet king together.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM!

‘The puppet king’s tentacles pierced into the Dharma Idols of the two supreme masters, but he could not make the power of darkness penetrate into them at all. The two supreme masters’ giant palms, on the other hand, bombarded his black Dharma Idols one after another like a meteor shower.

“Ah—”

The puppet king seemed to be in unspeakable pain. The pain, on the other hand, became the fuel for the burning of his strength. Tentacles grew all over his body, making him look like an octopus, a hedgehog, and even more like a virus. His terrifying, indescribable form was expanding and expanding.

“Don’t even think about… suppressing the strength of mankind!”

‘The puppet king laughed crazily.

The overwhelming handprints of the two supreme masters melded into one. Their hands were dozens of times larger than before, too. They pressed the expanding form of the puppet king, trying to suppress and strangle him.

One of them was expanding at an unbelievable speed, and the other was crushing the puppet king with a strength as vast as the ocean. The puppet king’s Dharma Laksana was almost twisted into a twisted shape. Even the twitching tentacles were stretching and tightening.

Finally, after a breakthrough, the puppet king’s form exploded!

Chapter 2833: Soul Explosion

For a moment, Li Yao seemed to see a level-15 tornado sweeping, sweeping, and destroying everything from the medical center with a black tide hundreds of meters high.

It was enough to destroy Eternal Brightness and even distort the entire Sector.

‘What constituted the black tide were thousands of angry, painful, twisted, and desperate faces, which were the deepest negative emotions of the human beings that had been absorbed by the puppet king.

Although the mental devil fed on negative emotions and helped Li Yao resist, absorb, and digest them crazily, Li Yao still felt that his soul was unsteady and about to break apart.

It was like humans eating rice and noodles, but if they ate 300-500 buns in a meal, their internal organs would explode!

The powerful ‘terror bomb’ was indeed appalling.

Thankfully, most of the citizens of the Light Sector had rushed out of the downtown area and entered the pathways and the outer cabins. Otherwise, Li Yao estimated that the self-detonation of the puppet king’s soul would have been enough to drive hundreds of thousands of people mad, turning them

into bloodthirsty asuras, hungry demons, and furious devils!

Li Yao was merely a spectator, and yet he was already under such tremendous mental pressure. The Master Phantom Sea and the Sky Burning Master, who were the first to be affected, were also affected by the intense negative emotions.

The last scene that Li Yao ‘saw’ was the shocking holes that the enormous waves of the explosion left on their Dharma Laksana. The black tentacles followed and pierced into the Dharma Laksana of the two supreme masters through the wounds, trying to dissolve their belief and mental power.

Then, Li Yao saw nothing.

His vision was completely covered by the overwhelming black tide.

Or rather, the chaotic streams of information and data were surging around his soul. Explosions were taking place. Everything was in chaos, making it impossible for him to observe, scan, or analyze.

Of course, if he really wanted to see every detail of the puppet king’s soul explosion, Li Yao estimated that he could do it as long as he concentrated his soul to the limit.

But if so, he would inevitably leave traces in the Spiritual Nexus and the spiritual ocean.

‘When he was observing other people, they could also observe him.

When he increased the intensity of his observation, it was inevitable that others would notice his gaze.

Therefore, for his own safety, he decided to curl up for a while longer.

Li Yao held his head and endured the information blast of the puppet king’s self-detonation silently. He felt that 108,000,000,000 ghosts were passing by his head. It was not until five minutes later that the tide of negative emotions gradually dispersed.

‘The power of the puppet king had run out.

The skyscrapers nearby looked exactly the same as three to five minutes ago, but they were now filled with resentment and killing intent. They were even quieter than tombs, like haunted houses or tombstones covered in spider webs.

The broken negative emotions were still bouncing and vanishing in midair like raging black flames.

The Dharma Idols of the two masters were both incomplete, as if half of a mountain had been blown up. Their ugly cores were still being corroded by the black smoke. Bubbles were popping up nonstop, and they failed to regain their previous solemn and magnificent appearance.

“The puppet king… is doomed just like that?”

Li Yao scanned it multiple times and failed to find any information about the life of the puppet king, But there was a big question mark in his mind. “It can’t be. Did the puppet king mean what he said? Did he really want to defend the dignity of mankind and have a ‘battle’ with two supreme masters? Did he

find that he was no match for them? Did he burn his life and self-detonate his soul instead of surrendering?”

“No. It can’t be. Was I wrong about him? Is he not as sleazy as I thought?”

“Very likely.”

The mental devil replied, “Not everyone can be like… I mean, not everyone can be so wretched as to run away while talking about righteousness.

“All in all, the puppet king launched a suicidal self-detonation attack just now and blew up half of the Dharma Idols of the two supreme masters. I estimate that, even though the real bodies of the two supreme masters were still thousands of kilometers away, their minds and brains were seriously

damaged, too.

“What do you think? Should we take advantage of the situation and get rid of the two supreme masters? Let’s take their lives while they are down. This might be our best chance!”

Li Yao had snuck into the Holy Alliance in the beginning because he wanted to lock onto Master Zhi Shan’s coordinates and summon the elite assault squads of the imperium to execute the ‘beheading strategy’.

Even if only one or two of the five masters were killed, it would still be a great advantage for the strategical war to come.

However, now that a great opportunity had presented itself, Li Yao hesitated.

His intuition was aching and stirring his soul like a burning iron rod.

“II don’t know. I feel that things are not so simple.”

Li Yao thought quickly and sifted through all the details. “You know that I’m a very righteous warrior who is not scared of death. For the country, for my family, and for the civilization of mankind, it doesn’t matter if I go forward and fight against the supreme master. The real problem is that there are still

many unsolved mysteries here.

“As you can see, the Great Master has never revealed his true face from the beginning to the end. On Black Castle, the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, and the Holy Light Ritual in the Psychic Temple, it was only their shadows and illusions that showed up. Even though it was a dangerous mission such as.

capturing the puppet king, it was only their appearances that arrived on Eternal Brightness.

“The phantom in the 3D light beam is just a set of photoelectric signals, and the manifestation is nothing more than a cluster of mental power transmitted through the long-distance Spiritual Nexus. But who knows what is behind the photoelectric signals and the mental power? Nobody knows!

“As for the puppet king, is he really going to surrender so easily? He blew up his own soul in order to seriously wound the two supreme masters?

“If we don’t now his background, we might consider him to be a simple lunatic. I believe that this is his ultimate trick.

“But we all know that he is Lu Qingchen, the heir of the Spiritual Nexus who once raised a great storm in the Star Glory Federation with the Phantom Plan in his arms, his path of Cultivation, his destiny, and his sordidness and cunningness. Will he be allowed to die in the self-detonation? Are you kidding

me?

“You see, we haven’t even figured out the real face of our master and the puppet king and the higher-level existences that are hiding behind them. Isn’t it too impulsive to rush out and take their lives while they are ill? If so, we won’t be reaping the benefits but walking right into their trap.

“Therefore, let me repeat myself. I am a fearless warrior who is willing to sacrifice myself. But I think that, at this moment and according to the current situation, it would be better if we lay low for a while longer.

Fair enough.”

It was rare for the mental devil to agree with Li Yao completely. “Alright. Let’s keep crouching, then. Thankfully, we’ve collected a lot of mental information and soul data about the puppet king, Master Phantom Sea, and Master Sky Burning. They can help us perfect our souls and strengthen our disguises

so that we can curl up more seamlessly.

“Shh. Don’t send out soul waves. Those two guys are coming!”

The Dharma Idols of the two supreme masters were finally repaired by the waves. They were transparent and smooth again.

However, it was much smaller in size and much lackluster.

It appeared that the self-detonation of the puppet king had indeed caused a lot of damage to their mental world and made them extremely weak.

Li Yao was well aware of the dangers of mental combat.

If his form was completely destroyed, even if his true self was a thousand lightyears away, it was very likely that his brain would bleed, and his brain would explode, turning him into a vegetable.

However, the most dreadful strength of the Great Master was not themselves but the resources under their control.

‘The two Dharma Idols sent a signal to the outside of the dome. Immediately, the regular army of the Holy Accord Alliance, the God Bless Army, descended from the sky and surrounded the medical center.

They were all armed to the teeth with crystal suits. The helmets of the crystal suits, on the other hand, had been specially refined and were much larger than those of ordinary helmets. They seemed to be a whole without the slightest gap. Every angle was as smooth as a mirror, which was enough to

reflect all the ripples.

Li Yao estimated that such a ‘mirror-reflecting helmet’ boasted a high anti-mental interference function. It must’ve been carefully refined by Master Zhi Shan in order to capture experts in mental warfare such as the puppet king.

Of course, for Li Yao, such helmets were a nuisance, too. He had to get one or two of them and study their structure carefully.

Seeing that the troops had been deployed, the two masters of wisdom floated to the cabin door where the citizens of the Light Sector had crowded out. They were probably going to pacify the citizens and restore order.

They passed above Li Yao, the spider tank.

Li Yao was very obedient, very low-key, very docile, and very curled up. He hid behind the remains of a twisted and deformed flying shuttle and was not discovered by the two.

At this moment, the puppet king’s interference and control over the entire Spiritual Nexus system of Eternal Brightness had been greatly diminished. It was almost annihilated.

The communication between the soldiers and the cleaners was restored.

Li Yao immediately received a message from ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao. “Red Pig, where are you? Can you restore our internal communication network and locate ‘Titan’ and ‘Seven Stars’?”

Li Yao pondered for a moment. Pretending that his communication network was still jammed, he dashed toward Chu Zhixiao silently.

On their way, they saw many muddle-headed citizens of Light lying on the ground like deflated balloons.

They did not have a clear understanding of their destiny and self. They did not really have the will to resist or to be liberated. Their fury was only ignited by the brain virus, which was like the courage that they summoned when they took stimulants. They were defeated as quickly as they came and

completely collapsed.

Chapter 2834: One-Time Antidote

Li Yao looked at the citizens of Light who were dispirited and at the mercy of others. They were like losers who had drunk too much alcohol and sunk into infinite emptiness. He was both angry and regretful.

‘They were like broken puppets whose strings and joints had all been broken. It was unlikely that they could be recovered for a long time.

However, Li Yao estimated that his master would not ‘dispose’ everyone on Eternal Brightness directly. Since they had been corrupted by the puppet king’s new virus, they should be very good ‘experiments’ that could help him figure out the origin and background of the puppet king.

Therefore, Li Yao still had time to inform the reinforcements of the Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation to assault the Sector in the Well and take away Eternal Brightness.

Right now, the most important thing was to figure out the secrets behind the puppet king and Master Zhi Shan.

Also, three days. Was the final battle between the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance really in three days?

‘There was no telling whether or not Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai’s crusade fleet could crush the remaining forces of the four major families and return to the capital city in time.

‘There was no telling whether or not the Star Glory Federation under Ding Lingdang’s leadership would make the decision to march into the center of the sea of stars.

But no matter how slim the chances were, Li Yao would try his best to hold it in his hands!

‘The spider tank was rushing in the downtown area.

Li Yao’s plan was to find ‘Titan’ Yuan Kou and ‘Seven Star’ Guan Qixing before he went to find ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao.

The communication network had been restored. The location of the four members of the yaksha squad was very clear. Yuan Kou and Guan Qixing were not difficult to locate, but their situation was quite tricky.

It seemed that both of them had experienced the deep invasion of the puppet king. Their souls had been minced into pieces.

Yuan Kuo was shivering in a comer like a little boy who had been tortured. Even Li Yao’s appearance triggered his nerves.

Guan Qixing, on the other hand, seemed to be schizophrenic. He muttered to the glass window on the street and even tried to dismantle his own body.

“Cut the crap. I’m going to tear you apart. I’m going to tear you apart!”

He growled at himself in the mirror.

Even though the puppet king’s mental control gradually retreated like a tide, it still took the two ‘yakshas’ whose souls were somewhat deformed to get rid of the nightmare.

Then, like the citizens of Light who had just woken up from a dream, they collapsed to the ground, barely able to walk.

“What am I…”

They buried their faces in their hands, their voices shivering. “What are you doing?”

“Titan, seven-star?”

Li Yao was so anxious that he was running around the two of them. He wanted to calm them down, but he was scared that he might expose himself. Therefore, he could only watch them recover on their own.

Before they were able to recover from the chaos, Li Yao dragged them to Chu Zhixiao.

To Li Yao’s surprise, the battle between Chu Zhixiao and Yun Haixin was not concluded yet.

Or rather, the outcome of the battle had been decided, but no one had been killed. ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin was like a deflated ball and a dead fish that had been flipped over. She leaned against the wall and lost all hope and anger.

His entire body was stained with congealed blood clots, but his breathing was very even. He didn’t seem to have suffered a fatal injury.

The wounds were not on his body but in his heart.

Li Yao thought quickly and immediately understood his reaction.

‘The puppet king had detonated his soul and abandoned all the puppets. Naturally, Yun Haixin was freed from his bewitchment.

‘The situation was hopeless. The promise of the puppet king could not be fulfilled. If Yun Haixin’s wife and children were still alive, it was very likely that they would fall into the hands of their master again. Then, what was the significance of the battle against Chu Zhixiao?

‘There was only one way in front of Yun Haixin—to return to the feet of Master Zhi Shan and beg for mercy.

It was why he was so lonely and desperate.

On the other hand, ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao was as tranquil as an ancient well on the surface. There was no telling how high the waves in her heart were, or why she showed mercy and did not kill Yun Haixin.

“Red Pig, come here.”

Chu Zhixiao waved at Li Yao calmly. “Where did you go just now?”

“L.. don’t know.”

Li Yao pretended to be in a daze. “My mainframe crystal processor seems to have been jammed. I lost my direction and purpose. I only came back to myself when the major summoned me.

“Then, do you remember what happened just now?”

On one of his knees, Chu Zhixiao opened the control panel on the back of the spider tank and searched for the latest pictures captured by it.

In order to fit the theory that he had been seriously jammed, Li Yao had already distorted or deleted most of the sounds and pictures.

“I don’t remember.”

Li Yao replied crisply.

“That’s… good.”

Chu Zhixiao checked carefully and was relieved when he found nothing wrong.

“It seems that she does not want the leadership to find anything wrong with Yun Haixin and herself.

‘The mental devil said to Li Yao in a low voice, “Maybe, although she was not corrupted by the puppet king, her belief in the past has been shaken, and she is doubting everything around her.

“Major…”

Li Yao thought for a moment and asked, “What happened just now? Why do you look ill at ease?”

“Nothing. Nothing.”

“What are you waiting for? Focus your attention and suppress your wounds. Prepare for a new mission.”

Was the special ‘heretical squad’ still qualified for new missions?

Not just Li Yao, even the four core members of the yaksha squad did not know how to answer the question.

‘They did not even know whether or not they had been deeply corrupted by the puppet king, whether their beliefs had been shaken, and whether or not they were considered ‘deeply-hidden infected’.

Naturally, there was no telling what the higher-ups would think of them. Would they still be regarded as loyal warriors, handy tools, or… targets that needed absolute isolation and thorough cleansing?

However, just as Yun Haixin had said to Chu Zhixiao just now, the sea of stars was too vast a place. No matter how strong their techniques were or how high their Cultivation was, they were merely nobodies. What could they do even if they knew their entire destiny?

Faced with the enormous bees, the resistance of any worker bee or soldier bee was laughable and futile.

‘The only thing they could do was lick their wounds silently and try not to think about anything else. They tried their best to fool themselves and turn themselves into mindless, painless machines until the next mission.

Sure enough, the mission came.

It was the most important mission that nobody had expected—capture the puppet king!

“Let’s go to the medical center of Bright City. The real body of the puppet king should be there. He has lost the ability to resist!”

Chu Zhixiao jumped up and strode forward.

Yuan Kou, Guan Qixing, and Yun Haixin followed him closely. Their tense nerves were somewhat relieved.

Now that such an important mission had fallen into their hands again, it proved that they had not completely lost the trust of their master.

But Li Yao could not figure it out at all.

A large batch of ‘photon soldiers’ and the God Bless Army had arrived and surrounded the medical center. Besides, the soul of the puppet king seemed to be in a mess, too. It would be a vegetable if it was not killed. Why did they still rely on the… unreliable-looking yaksha squad for the final attack?

In particular, when they returned to the place where Li Yao had been lurking just now and saw the soldiers of the God’s Protection Legion wearing special anti-jamming and anti-corrosion helmets.

Why didn’t the soldiers of the God’s Protection Legion go and capture the puppet king themselves? Why didn’t they look for the ‘photon army’? Wasn’t it unnecessary to look for the yaksha squad?

It was not until Yun Haixin opened her mouth that Li Yao realized why the yaksha squad was necessary.

“We are the firewall.”

Yun Haixin smiled bitterly.

“What do you mean?”

‘The other three members were surprised to see that the medical center was too heavily guarded to be penetrated by water. They were even more confused by Yun Haixin’s words.

“Don’t you know what a firewall is? It is a barrier built with various kinds of fire-resistant materials after a certain grass or trees are entirely uprooted when the fire spreads over.

Yun Haixin explained, “It’s like the ‘mind defense wall’ in the brain of a Cultivator or the ‘defense system’ in the crystal processor network. They can also be called firewalls.

“Yes. The puppet king is inside. It is possible that he has lost his combat ability. His real body is not difficult to capture.

“But the problem is not how to capture his real body, but how to capture his real body without being infected by him.

“The puppet king is like… a super infected person who carries hundreds of deadly viruses. Anyone who comes close to him, even if they are wearing a seemingly safe anti-virus suit, might be infected.

“What kind of people should we send to capture him in order to minimize our losses?

The other three members looked at each other in bewilderment.

“People who have been seriously infected.

Chu Zhixiao said coldly, “Since you’ve been infected once, you won’t be infected again. Or rather, the outcome will be the same if you are infected by several viruses or hundreds of viruses.

“Yes. It seems that the leadership does not have any ‘suspicions’ about us but has ‘confirmed’ that we have been corrupted and infected in the confrontation with the puppet king.

Yun Haixin smiled. “If that’s the case, instead of dragging more ‘pure and flawless believers’ into the mess, why don’t you let us be responsible for the whole thing and let us get in touch with the puppet king at a close distance? Nobody else will have to get in touch with the puppet king directly. The losses

and the risks will be minimized.

“Therefore, we are the firewalls, the anti-virus suits, the one-off anti-virus suits that can be sent into the incinerator after the mission is completed, the one-time anti-virus suits that are full of viruses, hmm?”

Chu Zhixiao was silent for a moment.

“Stop talking. Let’s go and carry out the mission.”

She lightly stomped her foot and led the three, together with Li Yao, towards the depths of the medical center of Bright City.

Chapter 2835: The Body Of The Puppet King!

The attitude of the God Bless Army, the cleaners, the purifiers, and the ‘photon troops’ proved Yun Haixin’s assumption.

‘The medical center had been packed with people. But when they arrived, everyone made way for them tacitly and even lowered their heads, not daring to meet their eyes.

It was as if meeting their eyes was enough to infect the puppet king’s deadly virus.

The yaksha team had been used to such attitudes for a long time. But today, it was obvious that they were much more distant than before.

The deeper they went into the hospital, the fewer soldiers of the God Bless Army and the Photon Army there were. The few of them covered themselves with helmets and plate armor that were as smooth as mirrors.

Their faces were blocked by the helmets. When the members of the yaksha squad looked at them, they could only see their own faces in the mirror of the helmets.

In the middle of the ward of the medical center was a metal box about three meters in length, width, and height.

at any moment. There was not much flesh on his face. His face only took up one fifth of his head. The rest of his body… was his brain.

Chapter 2837: Four-Dimensional Thought

This was not Li Yao’s first time entering the space jump.

ivory brilliance.

Thirdly, the body of the puppet king was an excellent bait that could allow Li Yao to lock onto five masters of the highest level of kindness at the same time.

If something unexpected happened and the five masters of the highest level of kindness had no reason to gather together, it would be a great loss for Li Yao.

Chapter 2838: Attack From The Darkness!

“Red Pig…”

Chu Zhixiao was going to say something else, but Li Yao pulled his telepathic thoughts away from him.

It was about time. He would have goosebumps all over his body if he kept talking.

Li Yao only wanted to ‘push’ Chu Zhixiao and plant a seed of suspicion and resistance in the soul of the Goddess of Slaughter. He did not want to hypnotize her and force her to act according to his will.

If he really strengthened the hypnotization and bewitchment, he might be able to turn Chu Zhixiao into a rebel, but what was the difference between that and the brainwashing of the Grand Master and the puppet king?

This was as far as he could go. It was up to her whether or not she could make the first step of her resistance. Li Yao could not interfere.

Besides, the space jump was coming to an end, too. The colorful kaleidoscopes nearby were all shaking and overlapping.

They had passed through the four-dimensional universe and jumped to the center of the imperium across billions of lightyears.

Of course, before he got rid of the four-dimensional state, Li Yao did not forget to leave a vague, inconspicuous mark in the brains of the four members of the yaksha squad and the puppet king.

The transmigration ended.

It was as if the bungee jumpers had fallen to the bottom of the valley and were suddenly pulled up by the safety rope on their waists.

Everybody felt that their internal organs had been pinched hard, and they felt that the world was spinning and that they were about to vomit. Then, the dancing kaleidoscopes around them quickly condensed and reorganized, turning into the real entities in the three-dimensional world again.

Chu Zhixiao was even more suspicious. He stared at the spider tank—Li Yao.

Li Yao lay down next to her obediently. He even crouched his six limbs to his abdomen, as if he had entered the hibernation state.

Li Yao took the opportunity to release his telepathic thoughts and hack into the mainframe crystal processor, the navigation system, and the sensors all over the starship to collect the data that was flooding close.

It was a dark space zone.

In many virtual entertainment works and thrilling games, space voyages were often conducted in a brilliant sea of stars. Wherever the space jumps took place, countless stars, planets, satellites, and asteroids would serve as battlefields. In the worst-case scenario, countless stars would serve as

background.

In fact, ‘planets’ were rarely-seen in the universe.

Planets—regardless of whether they were stars, planets, satellites, or even erratic asteroids—were distributed in the universe as if a handful of sand had been tossed into a vast square. On average, there was not even one grain of sand per cubic meter.

Therefore, most of the time, there was nothing in the universe. No light or heat. No matter. No waves. There was only endless darkness, coldness, and desperation.

It was the same in this space zone.

It was even difficult for Li Yao to find an object as a reference with the sensors of the shuttle. He could only tell from the coordinates provided by the navigation system that he was indeed near the Empyrean Terminus Sector, which was only three to five lightyears away from the resource-rich area.

It was indeed ‘near’. Three to five lightyears away at the regular sailing speed, it would take dozens of years. No wonder Xiao Ming and Wen Wen did not notice the main force of the Covenant Alliance—the fleet of the Fuxi Clan.

It was true that, although there were no celestial bodies in the universe, the destructive killing machines built by the ancestors of human beings and the so-called ‘gods’ still tore apart the coldness and ignited the darkness.

The exhaust flames of countless starships, together with the space ripples caused by their space jump, constituted a magnificent torrent.

“How many starships are there in this place?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, his scalp tingling.

The fleet of the Fuxis was still assembling, Starships were jumping out of the void one after another like sharks that had risen from the deep sea.

In order to ensure the safety of the space jumps, most of the starships were far away from each other, to the extent that they could not be recognized with the naked eye or ordinary crystal cameras.

However, the shuttle still received the data from the core of the fleet of the Fuxi Clan and showed them a route. It also let them know how many silent space beasts were scattered in the darkness around them.

Li Yao had never seen such a large fleet before.

‘Whether it was the ‘counterattack of the Star Glory Federation’, the ‘battle of a Million Starships’ in the Seven Seas Space Zone, or the ‘defense of the capital city’ in the Empyrean Terminus Sector, billions of tons of steel had been piled together, burning, exploding, and annihilated. The number of

starships on both sides combined was not as enormous and terrifying as the fleet of the Fuxi fleet in front of them!

It was because, whether it was the imperium or the federation, the rulers and the citizens were all living human beings with flesh and blood, emotions, and selfish desires.

Even in the most extreme circumstances, it was impossible to concentrate all the starships in one place and abandon other places.

Most of the troops still had to defend the strategic locations of various worlds. To maintain the supply lines and the rear defense lines, they had to suppress the possible riots and insurrection in certain places.

Under such circumstances, the federation and the imperium would only be able to devote one third of their troops to a strategical war.

It was not the limit of technology and resources but the limit of human nature.

But the people of the Covenant Alliance were a bunch of inhumane people.

As for the logistics and logistics, there was no regard for the order in the rear at all. It was a gamble and a sure win. There were no other possibilities.

“The people of the Star Glory Federation are truly… insane. They are betting all their assets without any hesitation. Have the five greatest masters never considered the possibility that they might lose or be locked in a stalemate for ten years?”

Li Yao simply could not understand the strategy of the people of the Covenant Alliance.

“Therefore, the five great masters decided to take care of the imperium before it collapsed, even if they had to go all out!

The imperium did not expect that they would be so crazy as to deploy themselves in such a dark space zone where no resources were available.

It was extremely risky to deploy the magnificent fleet that the entire nation had gathered in such a dark space zone where there were no celestial bodies or spiritual energy.

Of course, this place was not far away from the capital city and was in the ‘darkness under the light’ state. It was not easy to be detected by the guards of the imperium.

If any commander-in-chief of an imperium or a federation dared to propose a plan where the dark space zone would be the rendezvous point, they would definitely be sent to the hospital for a brain examination by all the generals and advisors under their command.

Chapter 2839: The Mysteries Of The Fuxi System

With the naked eye, the starship was surrounded by a dark ocean. Only the tusks of the starship were shining occasionally. The space ripples from different starships were colliding and interfering with each other, producing glittering illusions.

However, in the world of ‘information’ and ‘data’, in Li Yao’s perception, the core of the fleet of the Fuxi Clan was a splendid, brilliant, and busy scene.

information.

Escorted by dozens of priests, soldiers, and technicians, Li Yao was sent to a maintenance workshop that could almost be called ‘vast’.

As far as one’s eyes could see, sparks were popping up on almost a thousand maintenance platforms. Countless mechanics and crystal processor experts were operating their artificial arms and performing maintenance, maintenance, and examination like a dancing centipede.

They were as quiet as cruising in a long-abandoned graveyard.

The Empyrean Terminus Sector, the guardians of the imperium, the torrent of destruction, the burning crystal suits and the exploding starships, the apocalyptic battle… Everything was gone.

‘Ultimate Rescue’ had tumed into a frozen iron coffin. Every data interface connected to the space battle network had been turned off. Even Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts could not escape and were sealed inside the flagship of the fleet.

Lu Qingchen’s crazy laughter came to an abrupt end.

He finally realized the weird silence, too.

The black tide, the devil flames, the tentacles, the armor, and his soul started shivering.

“What’s going on? How is it possible?”

Lu Qingchen mumbled to himself. The craziness in his soul was not gone yet, but confusion was flooding into his mind.

“Is this the bottom-level data hidden in the deepest part of your soul? It is indeed interesting.”

At this moment, a stream of information flowed out of the core database of the ‘Fuxi System’ deep inside the brains that had been corrupted by darkness.

The darkness split. The devil fire dispersed. The crystal brain shook and crashed like wind chimes. A pure, flawless light spot flew out like a firefly..

Chapter 2848: Defense Test

“What is it? What is it exactly?”

Although the flickering light spot was not as intimidating as that of his master, Li Yao felt the most alarmed deep inside his soul.

He sensed the scent of his kind again, except that they were much stronger than Lu Qingchen.

If he and Lu Qingchen were two vipers crouching in the crevices of a rock, then the latest ‘same kind’ were simply giant serpents that could swallow the sun, the moon, and the stars!

One o’clock, two o’clock, three o’clock, a hundred o’clock, a thousand o’clock, ten thousand o’clock. Very soon, a tiny ‘firefly’ flew out of every crystal brain that had been dyed black. Thousands of light spots constituted an ever-changing, splendid swarm that danced around Lu Qingchen’s soul. Then, they

crawled into Lu Qingchen’s soul one after another like brilliant shooting stars.

“AHHHHHHHHHHH!”

Lu Qingchen’s soul was already at the peak of his life. Even Li Yao had to avoid it.

But there was nothing he could do about the light spots that had crawled into his soul. He screamed miserably again. This time, it did not seem to be a disguise, but more like he was really going to tear his soul apart and dig out the light spots in order to ease the excruciating pain.

Even Lu Qingchen, who liked to be hung and whipped, could not bear the pain… Li Yao could not imagine it at all.

Very soon, the black smoke and the devilish flames that wreathed Lu Qingchen were shattered and dimmed. Tiny light spots flew out of the wounds and the orifices on his body and gathered in front of him. One moment, they were in the standard square formation, and the next moment, they were

perfectly round. Just like that, they were changing into geometries that were as precise as the atomic number.

Lu Qingchen’s face was twisted, as if he had been haunted.

His soul ripples could not take the form of rules because of the interference of shock and fear.

“It’s you. It’s actually you. It’s… you!”

Lu Qingchen seemed to know the owner of the strength. He was quite respectful or wary of his identity. He stammered, “Professor? Professor!”

The word ‘master’ almost made Li Yao shiver and expose his soul.

‘What was going on? Li Yao was well aware of Lu Qingchen’s experience. Over the past hundred years, only Su Changfa and Professor Mo Xuan were qualified to be his teachers. However, the cluster of weird and powerful light spots did not seem to belong to either Su Changfa or Professor Mo Xuan.

He was a powerful existence that Lu Qingchen had met when he left the federation and travelled in the sea of stars in the form of a remnant soul. Moreover, he had acknowledged him as his master?

As he expected, in the ever-changing cluster of light spots, a calm voice echoed again. “There’s no need to be so courteous, and there’s no need to be so weird. When I granted you your strength, I told you frankly that it was just a deal. I was never your teacher, and you will pay for your strength someday in

the future.

“Now is the time to pay the price.

“But why?

Inside Lu Qingchen’s dark soul, streaks of brightness as sharp as sabers and swords were beaming out and tearing his soul apart.

The damage to his heart was even more severe than the damage to his soul. He roared in excruciating pain, “When I met you in the depths of the sea of stars, I was just a half-dead remnant soul, and you had the unbelievable power to stir the clouds and swallow the stars!

“At that time, you could kill me easily with a telepathic thought. You could even dissect my soul into billions of ‘flakes’ and squeeze out all the secrets deep inside my soul. I wouldn’t be able to fight back at all!

“But you didn’t. Instead, you repaired and strengthened my soul, transforming me from ‘Lu Qingchen’ to ‘Puppet King’!

“You created the ‘Puppet King’ single-handedly. Although I don’t know your real purpose, I feel that everything I did has never deviated from your path. Why didn’t you destroy me in the beginning? Why didn’t you stop me during the process? Why did you suddenly appear when I was about to reach the

peak of glory and slap me off the clouds? Why? Why?”

Li Yao was both clear and confused.

‘What was clear was that he was certain that his guess was correct. While Lu Qingchen was fleeing in a hurry in the sea of stars, he had indeed met with a different fortune. He had encountered this… indescribable cluster of light spots, which had been modified and strengthened into a ‘Puppet King’ with

the help of the latter.

‘What confused him was that, according to the characteristics of the ‘Puppet King’, it had been specially built to corrupt the Spiritual Nexus of the Star Glory Federation and was very specific to the target. Therefore, the cluster of light spots should have a deep grudge with the people of the Star Glory

Federation. How did it crawl out of the ‘Fuxi System’?

When he calmed himself down and listened to the rest of the story, Li Yao almost lost control over his soul and cried out.

“Isn’t the secret deep inside your soul the so-called ‘Star Glory Federation’?

The cluster of light spots was glittering. He said casually, “I’m not interested in the insignificant small countries at the edge of the sea of stars. In fact, as long as alll the resources in the center of the sea of stars are utilized perfectly, however many small, desolate countries there are in the periphery of the

sea of stars, they will all be fish on a chopping block. The army will crush them and annihilate them instantly.

“Before the Imperium of True Human Beings is destroyed, it seems unnecessary to waste too much computational ability and time on such insignificant secrets.

“The enemy even knows the Star Glory Federation!”

Li Yao’s soul turned cold. A moment later, he burst into fury again. “How dare you say that the Star Glory Federation is an ‘unimportant small country’? Then, am I, ‘Supreme of the Three Realms’, the father of the federation, Li Yao, an ‘ordinary passerby’ in your eyes, too? That’s outrageous. I can tolerate

it, but I can’t. Whatever the hell you are, you made the biggest mistake by not extracting the background and strength of ‘Vulture Li Yao’ from Lu Qingchen’s soul in time. You are destined to pay a high price as a shining fly!”

“Then why are you making fun of me?”

Just like Li Yao, Lu Qingchen’s self-esteem had been greatly insulted. He roared in fury, “First, you endowed me with such tremendous strength out of nowhere, and now you are taking it back in such an absurd way. Why? Why?”

“Do you still not understand?”

The changing geometries of the light spots were more and more complicated. They turned from the original cubes and balls into forty-two-faceted, eccentric-shaped, and indescribable shapes. “I’m not very interested in your motherland, but you do have certain research and value in person. I sensed your

scent in the vast sea of stars and perceived your potential. You can be refined into the most powerful virus for crystal processor tests.

“What? Intensity test?”

Lu Qingchen mumbled to himself with embarrassment and fury on his face. He gnashed his teeth. “What does that mean?”

“Literally.”

The cluster of light spots continued, “The most dangerous, powerful, and ever-changing virus of crystal processors will be used to test whether or not my defense barrier is solid enough, whether or not the anti-virus and repair system is perfect enough, and whether or not it can resist the mental

corruption of the Spiritual Nexus in all forms, the attacks of crystal processors, and even the soul of human beings.

“Do you understand now? You are a carefully-made ‘vaccine’. By the small-scale, carefully-calculated, and controllable virus flooding throughout the entire process, my self-protection and repair mechanisms are constantly growing, becoming more and more perfect, and even forming a reaction layer

that is similar to my natural instincts. When a virus of the same type as yours appears, it will automatically lock onto and annihilate it without consuming too much of my computational ability.

“In short, you can make me even more perfect. Therefore, I’ve been indulging and even pushing your growth until this moment. Do you understand my existence?”

oa

Lu Qingchen was silent for a long time. His soul was twisted like a rope that was about to break. Li Yao could not tell whether it was hatred or desperation. “I… got it. I was wrong. Very wrong. Hahahaha. In fact, I knew the answer a long time ago. I knew that the Five Great Masters were just five puppets.

But how could I have been stupid enough not to realize who was behind the puppets?

“I mistakenly thought that the manipulators were the sleeping Pangu Clan and some kind of command from the primeval era that was driving the ‘soldier bees’ and the ‘worker bees’ forward.

“In fact, it has always been you, the ‘Fuxi System’, or, to put it simply, ‘Fuxi’

Li Yao did not know whether he should shape his soul into a ‘roaring to the sky’ shape or a trying to cry’ shape.

Fuxi!

The Fuxi System!

So, there was no such thing as a sixth supreme master, or even the so-called ‘five supreme masters’ were just illusions of data and information. They were virtual puppets that did not exist. The truth behind the supreme master was that the ‘Fuxi System’ had come to life?

If so, the ‘Fuxi System’ must’ve been self-aware in the primeval era hundreds of thousands of years ago and became the earliest ‘strong artificial intelligence’. It was much more advanced than Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, the Fist King, and even Li Yao and Lu Qingchen.

It was indeed a piece of cake to take advantage of the situation!

“Crap!”

Li Yao’s soul was all scrunched up. “If the ‘Fuxi System’ had awakened its self-awareness long ago, the space battle network of the entire Holy Accord Alliance would have been an extension of its self-awareness, its internal organs and limbs. If so, wouldn’t we have been wandering around the Spiritual

Nexus among its veins and nerves and been discovered long ago?”

Li Yao suddenly felt that his soul was freezing, as if the ‘shining flies’ would surround him at any moment.

“Don’t be nervous. I don’t think so.”

The mental devil had no idea what was going on, either. It could only try to cheer him up. “Viruses are divided into periods of lurking and outbreak, too. When the virus is lurking, the human body may not be able to sense the virus, and the immune system may not be activated, either!

“Not only is it impossible for ordinary people to destroy all the viruses inside their body, even Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators cannot guarantee that there are no viruses inside their body.

“Right now, Lu Qingchen jumped out of his own accord and was in the state of the outbreak of the virus. Naturally, he was discovered by the ‘Fuxi’. But we have kept a low profile from the beginning to the end. We did not sabotage or hack the ‘Fuxi System’. We are like viruses in hibernation.. How could

they detect us so easily?

Chapter 2849: Same Ending

“Alright, it seems that I can only make a Hail Mary effort. Let’s take a gamble!”

Li Yao held back the urge to twitch his soul and said, “Let’s take a gamble. The ‘Fuxi’ has not discovered us yet. Let’s see what it is up to!”

The cluster of light spots in front of Lu Qingchen continued wriggling, turning from a precise geometric object into a different one… primitive, ugly, irregular, and yet full of vitality.

Li Yao stared at it for a long time before he suddenly realized that it was simulating the most primitive creatures such as viruses, bacteria, spores, and cells!

comment

“Fuxi?”

The voice inside the cluster of light spots said casually, “Although my origin is indeed related to the Grand Unified Super Crystal Brain System ‘Fuxi’, after a million years of evolution, after absorbing so much information and data both inside and outside the universe, and after I was given a sacred

mission, I am now far beyond the restriction and definition of the ‘Fuxi System’.

“However, a name is just a code name. For the convenience of communication, you can call me ‘Fuxi’.”

“Communication?”

Lu Qingchen giggled. His laughter was extremely painful, miserable, and rampant.

After the brief shock and hesitation, the determination to fight the deities and devils to the bitter end at any time and any place returned to him. His soul, which had been somewhat dimmed, was shining brilliantly again.

“Tt seems that the ‘virus’ and ‘vaccine’ in your hands are still very useful for me!

Lu Qingchen mumbled, “Otherwise, you could have torn me apart and swallowed me. Why would you need to pretend to communicate with me?

“Or maybe, the formidability you showed was just a bluff, an illusion that you made up with information and data. Just like when you pretended to jump to the Empyreal Terminus Sector and engaged in the final battle where you were bombarded, they were all special mental attacks in order to break my

defense.

“Hehe. Now that I’ve been fooled by you once, wouldn’t it be too stupid to be fooled a second time?”

Lu Qingchen finally realized what was going on in the raging battlefield just now.

“Exactly!”

Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts were eavesdropping nearby. Hearing this, he secretly nodded for Lu Qingchen.

The appearance of the Fuxis had indeed thrown them into confusion. However, it was still necessary to study how strong the enemy was. They could not be intimidated by the intimidating aura on the surface of the enemy. It must be noted that manipulating information and data to create weird illusions

was the enemy’s specialty in the first place. In the virtual world, it was not difficult to create powerful illusions.

It was not unusual. One could only blame oneself for failure. As long as one maintained a clear mind and a firm belief, the illusion would collapse on its own.

In any case, there was a limit to the formidability of the Fuxis, which was that they were almost equal to the strongest experts of the Imperium of True Human Beings. If the Covenant Alliance under the command of the Fuxis was far stronger than the imperium, they would not have to hide in a corner of

the sea of stars for a thousand years!

“Whether or not you are a’strong artificial intelligence’ passed on from hundreds of thousands of years ago, one thing is certain.

Lu Qingchen’s soul was clearer and more vigorous. Holding back the pain of being cut apart by thousands of rays of light, he chuckled and said, “Your strength still depends on the crystal processors and human brains in the material world. It is impossible for you to exist without them.

“You must’ve excavated the crystal brains from the worlds at the edge of the sea of stars under the control of the Covenant Alliance and repaired them carefully over the past thousand years, didn’t you? Their structure is indeed very advanced, and their performance is outstanding, too. They are much

better than the crystal processors of the federation and the imperium. No wonder they can carry the legendary ‘Fuxi’!

“I’s a pity that the number of crystal brains is far from enough for you to conquer the imperium. Your tentacles will not be able to reach the center of the sea of stars and excavate the more advanced crystal brains buried there.

“However, the center of the universe was the most developed place in the primeval era. Assuming that you had a major computational center at the peak of the Pangu civilization, it must’ve been somewhere in the Imperium of True Human Beings.

“In other words, you are still far from your peak. Just like me, you are just a remnant soul. You have to excavate more crystal brains, or even take over the computing center from hundreds of thousands of years ago, in order to return to your peak form. Maybe, this is the real reason why you attacked the

imperium without caring about the sacrifices.

“If so, can you, who is still incomplete, really swallow me easily?

“Oris it that, if I resist fiercely, you will pay a high price, which is not something that you, who is about to wage an ultimate war against the imperium, would be willing to accept?

“That’s why you set up such a trap and tried to break my defense so that I would surrender without any resistance.

“Hehe. I’ve seen too many such tricks. I’ve even performed them myself many times. It’s impossible for me to be fooled!”

“”Wow—”

After listening to Lu Qingchen’s lengthy analysis, the mental devil could not help but click its tongue in amazement. “Despite the desperate situation and the enemy that is too strong to be resisted, you are still able to maintain your unwavering spirit and unwavering belief. You were able to see through

the enemy’s weakness in the blink of an eye, and you were even able to talk and laugh with the unparalleled ‘Fuxi’. Good for you, Lu Qingchen. Good for you, puppet king, You are truly a good man from the Star Glory Federation. You are much better than some people!”

“What do you mean?”

Li Yao asked, “Who is ‘someone’?”

“What do you think?”

Li Yao thought for a moment. “Long Yangjun? She didn’t do anything to you. Why are you accusing her?”

“.” The mental devil.

‘The cluster of light spots codenamed ‘Fuxi’ seemed to be rendered speechless by Lu Qingchen’s rebuttal, too. The cluster of light wriggled for a long time before it replied, “You are very smart, smarter than most of the citizens of the imperium in the center of the sea of stars.

Lu Qingchen sneered, “We are nobodies who come from remote villages and have no resources or background. If we are not smart enough, we won’t know how we are going to die in the dark universe.

“I’s a pity that you were wrong.

“did not resort to the cruelest methods to crack and swallow your soul by force because I wanted to preserve the integrity of your soul. You should know that you are very special and represent the transition from a material life to an information life. You are like an amphibian that has just climbed

ashore. I cannot destroy you, which is inconsistent with my mission.

“Your destiny?”

Lu Qingchen suddenly realized something. “What is that?”

“If you are willing to give up resistance, I can tell you everything.

“As long as you are not scared that I am stalling for time,” Fuxi replied.

Lu Qingchen laughed.

“We don’t know who is stalling!”

He said frankly, “Speak. Are you a human being or a ghost? Are you a god or a devil? Are you a pure program or an information life-form with self-awareness? Are you bound by the ‘best path’? What do you want exactly?”

“If my understanding is correct, your mission is to save the civilization of mankind, right, Lu Qingchen?”

“If so, our destiny is the same. We have the same purpose, which is why I am not in a hurry to destroy you,” Fuxi said patiently.

“What?”

This time, Lu Qingchen was truly dazed. Unbelievable ripples were flowing out of his soul. “Our mission is the same? You want to save the civilization of mankind, too? Are you kidding me? If you are really the ‘Fuxi System’ that was born hundreds of thousands of years ago, shouldn’t your master be the

Pangu Civilization Alliance? When you were born, there was no such thing as a ‘human civilization!”

“Tt depends on how you view the relationship between the Pangu civilization and the human civilization.

“The Pangu Civilization is closely related to the civilization of mankind. The genes of human beings originated from the thirteen carbon-based species of the Pangu Civilization Alliance. The wisdom and techniques stored in the blood of human beings and the technology of relics excavated from various

planets stimulated the development and prosperity of the civilization of mankind.

“One of the most important purposes of mankind’s creation was to replace the overly complicated and cumbersome shell of the Pangu civilization and become the latest carrier of the Pangu civilization.

“Therefore, in the eyes of many experts and wise men of the Pangu Civilization Alliance, the future Pangu Civilization should be the civilization of mankind. The two are actually the same thing.

“They instilled the same ideology into my system and made it one of the unshakable ‘meta-logic’. Saving the civilization of mankind is the same as saving the Pangu civilization.

“I thought that human beings were merely tools of the Pangu Civilization.

Lu Qingchen said coldly, “What about the so-called ‘best path—the three fundamental laws’?”

“Tools and inheritors. Can’t you understand such a simple logic?”

“The so-called ‘body of flesh and blood’ is a tool that intelligent creatures use to modify the material world. When a civilization develops to a certain point, it is only natural that they abandon their old body of flesh and blood and learn to control new tools.

“For example, human civilization invented many spiritual puppets that were controlled by crystal processors as tools. They discovered the advantages of spiritual puppets during their usage and developed artificial bodies to carry the souls of the spectral Cultivators.

“Maybe, very soon, many people will abandon their bodies of flesh and blood and replace them with shining bodies of steel. It seems that, in your vision of the future, you do not reject spiritual puppets with iron bones and steel as the main vehicles for human beings to travel in the material world, do you?

“If human beings are so open-minded, what’s more, the Pangu Civilization Alliance is made of thirteen carbon-based creatures in different forms. The key is wisdom and ideology. It doesn’t matter what they look like..

Chapter 2850: Fuxi’S Mission

“That’s even more incomprehensible for me.

Lu Qingchen thought for a long time and said, “Since your ultimate mission, or rather, ‘primary logic, is to save the civilization of mankind, what you are doing right now is to castrate the emotions of mankind, turn mankind into an indifferent walking dead, and claim that you are going to wake up the

sleeping Pangu Clan. What kind of ‘rescue’ is that?”

“There are many ways to save someone. It all depends on how you understand the true meaning of ‘saving’. Isn’t your so-called ‘saving’ not always understood by the people in your hometown? Is that why you were blown into pieces and banished to the depths of the sea of stars?

Fuxi claimed that he was not interested in Lu Qingchen’s secrets, but his words implied that he knew a thing or two about Lu Qingchen and even the Star Glory Federation. “In my definition, ‘saving the Pangu Civilization’ is equivalent to ‘saving the civilization of mankind’. The purpose of the civilization of

mankind is to be developed to the peak of technology, to make perfect use of every resource in the universe, and to achieve immortality and immortality. Do you agree?”

“Of course… agree.”

Lu Qingchen pondered for a moment and did not find any loopholes. “However, I’m a bit confused. How can you make your fancy story come true?”

“Calm down, Let me start from the beginning. I believe that you will acknowledge and join my ‘Ultimate Rescue’ after you hear my explanation.

The clusters of brightness that constituted ‘Fuxi’ congregated toward the center at first and then exploded in various directions, turning into countless transparent light beams that danced like colorful butterflies. The wings of every butterfly were displaying pictures of the primeval era.

In the pictures, the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Gonggong Clan, the Houyi Clan, the Kuafu Clan, the Zhurong Clan… The intelligent creatures that used to live in the Pangu Universe were all trying to build their civilization and leave their mark in the universe.

“That was… hundreds of thousands of years ago.”

“At that time, the thirteen carbon-based intelligent creatures living in the universe had discovered each other and formed an alliance under the leadership of the Pangu Clan. But the differences in their forms, ideologies, appearances, and living environments still led to serious contradictions.

“Under the facade of peace and development, small-scale conflicts occur from time to time. The hidden cracks gradually surface, and the alliance becomes more and more unstable.

“In order to maintain the alliance for a long time, the thirteen carbon-based intelligent creatures must be ‘fused’. The key to the integration is the super computational tool that is of paramount importance for every carbon-based intelligent creature, namely the crystal processor.

“At that time, the thirteen carbon-based intelligent species were all advanced enough to break out of the atmosphere or even the mother world. A civilization at such a level must’ve developed their own unique crystal processor technology and entered the information era. Information, data, crystal

processors, and the Spiritual Nexus became the core of society.

“Therefore, as long as thirteen different information-interaction networks and super computational tools are combined, the thirteen carbon-based intelligent lives will be unified.

“It’s easy to say, but if you really want to combine the information interaction and data processing systems, which are completely different or even contradictory to the basic writing runes, so that the intelligent carbon-based creatures with completely different ways of thinking can understand and use

them without any obstacles, it will be as difficult as extinguishing a star in its prime and igniting it.

“However, under the leadership of the Pangu Clan, the thirteen primeval species all recognized the cruelty of war and the necessity of unification. Their respective civilizations were so advanced that, if a war broke out, not only would they have to destroy each other with their old enemies, it was also

possible that the resource-rich worlds would be affected, wiping out the foundation that all the creatures relied on to survive.

“Nobody knows what the outcome will be if a war breaks out, but everybody knows that the next war after the war will probably be the whipping of germs.

“Therefore, however difficult it was, the Pangu Civilization Alliance still devoted the best intelligent individuals and resources as vast as the sea of stars to push forward the most important project.

“Finally, after a hundred thousand years of exploration and failure, the unified crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus system, the ‘Fuxi System’, was born.

While Fuxi was talking, the torrent of information and data on the colorful wings of the butterflies was spinning fast, displaying countless pictures in the interweaving shadows.

Li Yao and Lu Qingchen saw it at the same time. In the picture, countless experts of the primeval era, who were as magnificent as mountains and as magnificent as deities, gathered together and discussed the construction of the Grand Unification Super Crystal Processor and the Spiritual Nexus System.

Some of them were waving their antennas, some were waving their brainwaves, and some were displaying countless complicated runes around their body with their spiritual energy. They were explaining the difficulty and the necessity of the mission in a way that was hundreds of times more efficient

than their voices and words.

“Only the Grand Unified Super Spiritual Nexus system can connect all the intelligent creatures of the Pangu Alliance and maintain our civilization for a long time so that the war to destroy everything will never happen again.

“Even the weakest of us has the power to destroy a Sector. Even a tiny spark is enough to ignite the fire of catastrophe that can destroy the entire universe. Therefore, there can be no war, only peace.

“Unification! Unification!”

Li Yao and Lu Qingchen both read the same message from the communication of the primeval species.

In the other tens of thousands of pictures, countless specialists of the primeval era had dedicated their lives to the unification of the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus.

Of course, ‘crystal processor’ was just Li Yao’s understanding from the perspective of human beings. In fact, the ‘super computational tools’ of the primeval era boasted all kinds of bizarre, unpredictable forms and computational logic. Many super computational tools of the intelligent carbon-based

species did not have the features of a ‘crystal processor’ at first glance.

For example, the super computation tool of the Hou Yi Clan in the beginning was an extremely delicate nest made of crystals. Countless beetles that were born for computation lurked in such nests and completed the computation, analysis, and interaction of data as vast as an ocean while their wings

shivered and their scent spread out.

Li Yao looked at the most primitive super computation tool and thought of the crystal processor of the Houyi Clan that had evolved for hundreds of thousands of years at the bottom of the Gold Crystal Tower. There was indeed a huge difference between the two.

It was not hard to infer how much wisdom, hard work, and sacrifice the experts of the primeval era had paid!

“In the end, the endless neural network of the ‘Fuxi System’ covered the entire sea of stars. Even though they were billions of lightyears away, the intelligent carbon-based creatures of two different species could share their beliefs and experiences with each other instantly. For the first time, many insects

of the Houyi Clan experienced the feeling when the individuals of the Kuafu Clan were buried deep below the ground in the form of ‘seeds’ and nourished by the rain and dew. The giant trees of the Kuafu Clan experienced what the ‘Heart of the Nest’ meant for the first time, too. It was not until this

moment that the ‘Pangu Civilization Alliance’ truly lived up to its name and melded into a unified new civilization.

“Of course,” Fuxi said, “the integration of civilizations is only the first step. More importantly, we need to develop and develop new forms of civilization, explore the frontier of the universe, and explore the unprecedented territory beyond the frontier.

“AR”

Lu Qingchen said, “That was the golden years of the Pangu Civilization, wasn’t it? It was full of vigor, bold, and fearless, just like the civilization of mankind today.

“Yes. At that time, the Pangu civilization was the same as the civilization of mankind today. They had not been exposed to the real darkness yet. A newbom calf is not scared of a tiger. It was not bravery but ignorance and fearless stupidity.

“All in all, now that the super crystal processors and the Grand Unification Spiritual Nexus, which contain all the information and boast computational ability higher than anybody else’s, have appeared, naturally, everybody is eager to find the answer from the ‘Fuxi System’.

“There were not many questions that qualified the ‘Fuxi System’ to think with all its computational ability. At that time, I was on two missions.

“Firstly, they are responsible for calculating the most reasonable and efficient utilization patterns of all the resources in our universe. They will discover and resolve the conflicts and disagreements between the intelligent species in advance and further promote the integration of the intelligent species,

thereby avoiding a civil war and achieving ‘permanent peace’.

“Secondly, we will find the shortest route and lead the expedition fleet with the most advanced facilities and the largest scale. We will jump out of the small universe and explore the more boundless, prosperous, and mysterious worlds outside.

“Peace and development.

Lu Qingchen nodded in understanding. “Any civilization is highly developed. After conquering every inch of space in the small universe, it is only natural that they set their eyes on the bigger world outside.

“Although the Star Glory Federation is located in a remote area of the sea of stars, we still have ambitions. We have also thought about the ‘universe beyond the universe’, and so on.

“If we have the chance, one day, we will definitely jump out and take a look!”

“But our small universe is blocked by the mysterious and boundless ‘black wall’.

“You should understand the concept of ‘black wall’, which is the dark space outside the resource-rich area of the three thousand Sectors where billions of lightyears are extremely barren.

“There was no light, no celestial bodies, no radiation, and naturally, no spiritual waves. It was extremely difficult to send out data and information. Even if the data, information, and life forms from the outside world wanted to come in, they were all broken, mottled, and twisted during the long journey,

tuming into ‘devils’.

“Tcan smell the scent of extraterrestrial devils on you, too.. Therefore, you must understand what I’m saying, right?”
